    46	export interface Snap {
    47	  A: string;
    48	  B: string;
    49	  C: string;
    50	  P: string;
    51	  E: string;
    52	  S: string;
    53	  matter: string;
    54	  energy: string;
    55	  reserveB: number;
    56	  reserveE: number;
    57	  heatAnalytic: string;
    58	  living: number;
    59	}
    60	export function livingCount(s: WorldState): number {
    61	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    62	  let k = 0;
    63	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if ((s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) !== 0) k++;
    64	  return k;
    65	}
    66	export function snap(s: WorldState, a: Audit): Snap {
    67	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
    68	  return { A: `${t.A}`, B: `${t.B}`, C: `${t.C}`, P: `${t.P}`, E: `${t.E}`, S: `${t.S}`, matter: `${t.matter}`, energy: `${t.energy}`, reserveB: a.reserveB, reserveE: a.reserveE, heatAnalytic: `${a.heatAnalytic}`, living: livingCount(s) };
    69	}
    70	
    71	export interface TransferRecord {
    72	  op: string;
    73	  before: Snap;
    74	  after: Snap;
    75	  detail: Record<string, number | string>;
    76	  /** Both exactly "0" for a valid record. */
    77	  residual: string;
    78	  matterResidual: string;
    79	}
    80	
    81	/** Run one mutation of (s, a) and record before/after; a nonzero residual or a failed validateState throws. */
    82	export function audited(op: string, s: WorldState, a: Audit, fn: () => Record<string, number | string>): TransferRecord {
    83	  assertAudit(s, a, `${op} (before)`);
    84	  const before = snap(s, a);
    85	  const detail = fn();
    86	  const errs = validateState(s);
    87	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`${op}: validateState: ${errs[0]}`);
    88	  const r = residuals(s, a);
    89	  if (r.energy !== 0n || r.matter !== 0n) throw new NicheInvalid(`${op}: nonzero residual (energy ${r.energy}, matter ${r.matter})`);
    90	  return { op, before, after: snap(s, a), detail, residual: `${r.energy}`, matterResidual: `${r.matter}` };
    91	}
    92	
    93	/** Split `total` evenly over `cells` (floor share, remainder to the first cells in order) and take it from channel `ch`. */
    94	export function debitEven(s: WorldState, ch: number, total: number, cells: readonly number[]): void {
    95	  if (total === 0) return;
    96	  const m = cells.length;
    97	  if (m === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("no cell can fund the reserve");
    98	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    99	  const base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
   100	  for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) {
   101	    const share = base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0);
   102	    if (s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]] < share) throw new NicheInvalid(`underfunded: cell ${cells[j]} holds ${s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]]} < ${share} of channel ${ch}`);
   103	  }
   104	  for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) s.cells[ch * n + cells[j]] -= base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0);
   105	}
   106	
   107	/**
   108	 * Move `dB` quanta of lattice nutrient (matter) and `dEnergy` units of lattice free energy into the reserve. The reserve gains
   109	 * B = dB (valued eB each) and E = dEnergy - (eB - eA) * dB, so the funded reserve energy is exactly what left the lattice.
   110	 */
   111	export function moveToReserve(s: WorldState, a: Audit, dB: number, dEnergy: number, from: readonly number[]): void {
   112	  const { eA, eB } = s.cfg;
   113	  const e = dEnergy - (eB - eA) * dB;
   114	  if (e < 0) throw new NicheInvalid("reserve energy would be negative");
   115	  debitEven(s, CH.A, dB, from);
   116	  debitEven(s, CH.E, dEnergy, from);
   117	  a.reserveB += dB;
   118	  a.reserveE += e;
   119	}
   120	
   121	/** Start an audit for `s` as it is (nothing off the lattice yet). */
   122	export function openAudit(s: WorldState): Audit {
   123	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
   124	  return { energyStart: t.energy + s.heatOut - s.lightIn, matterStart: t.matter, reserveB: 0, reserveE: 0, heatAnalytic: 0n };
   125	}
   126	
   127	/** Fund the reserve (Br, Er) from the lattice: A down by Br, E down by (eB - eA) * Br + Er, over `from`. */
   128	export function fundReserve(s: WorldState, a: Audit, Br: number, Er: number, from: readonly number[]): TransferRecord {
   129	  return audited("fund-reserve", s, a, () => {
   130	    moveToReserve(s, a, Br, (s.cfg.eB - s.cfg.eA) * Br + Er, from);
   131	    return { reserveB: Br, reserveE: Er, cells: from.length };
   132	  });
   133	}
   134	
   135	const livingAt = (s: WorldState, i: number): boolean => {
   136	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   137	  return (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) !== 0;
   138	};
   139	
   140	/** Builder removal: living cells become non-living (lineage and genome cleared); B, P, E, A, C, S stay in place. */
   141	export function relabelLiving(s: WorldState, a: Audit): TransferRecord {
   142	  return audited("relabel-living", s, a, () => {
   143	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   144	    let cells = 0, B = 0;
   145	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   146	      if (!livingAt(s, i)) continue;
   147	      cells++;
   148	      B += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
   149	      for (let g = 0; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) s.genome[g * n + i] = 0;
   150	      s.cells[CH.MOT * n + i] = MOT_ZERO;
   151	    }
   152	    return { cells, relabelledB: B };
   153	  });
   154	}
   155	
   156	/** Ablation: every structural polymer quantum becomes waste C; the released energy (eP - eC) * P is booked as heatAnalytic. */
   157	export function convertPolymer(s: WorldState, a: Audit): TransferRecord {
   158	  return audited("polymer-to-waste", s, a, () => {
   159	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   160	    let P = 0;
   161	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   162	      const p = s.cells[CH.P * n + i];
   163	      P += p;
   164	      s.cells[CH.C * n + i] += p;
   165	      s.cells[CH.P * n + i] = 0;
   166	    }
   167	    a.heatAnalytic += BigInt(s.cfg.eP - s.cfg.eC) * BigInt(P);
   168	    return { convertedP: P, heatAnalytic: `${BigInt(s.cfg.eP - s.cfg.eC) * BigInt(P)}` };
   169	  });
   170	}
   171	
   172	/** Deterministic permutation of 0..n-1 from (seed, channel) alone, never from cell contents, so every history is scrambled the same way. */
   173	export function permutation(n: number, seed: number, ch: number, mix: (x: number) => number): number[] {
   174	  const p = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i);
   175	  for (let j = n - 1; j > 0; j--) {
   176	    const r = mix((seed ^ Math.imul(ch + 1, 0x9e3779b1) ^ Math.imul(j + 1, 0x85ebca6b)) >>> 0) % (j + 1);
   177	    const t = p[j];
   178	    p[j] = p[r];
   179	    p[r] = t;
   180	  }
   181	  return p;
   182	}
   183	
   184	/** Independently permute the listed channels over all lattice cells; each channel total is preserved exactly, structure is not. */
   185	export function scrambleChannels(s: WorldState, a: Audit, seed: number, channels: readonly number[], mix: (x: number) => number, op = "scramble"): TransferRecord {
   186	  return audited(op, s, a, () => {
   187	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   188	    for (const ch of channels) {
   189	      const p = permutation(n, seed, ch, mix);
   190	      const old = s.cells.slice(ch * n, (ch + 1) * n);
   191	      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[ch * n + i] = old[p[i]];
   192	    }
   193	    return { channels: channels.join(",") };
   194	  });
   195	}
   196	
   197	/** A cell that may take receiver inoculum: nothing bound, no lineage, and room in E. `allowP` is for the hand-wall floor only. */
   198	export function placeable(s: WorldState, i: number, perE: number, allowP = false): boolean {
   199	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   200	  return s.cells[CH.B * n + i] === 0 && (allowP || s.cells[CH.P * n + i] === 0) && !livingAt(s, i) && s.cells[CH.E * n + i] + perE <= POOL_MAX;
   201	}
   202	
   203	/** Cells that are placeable in every state of the block (the intersection), in raster order. */
   204	export function commonCells(states: readonly WorldState[], disc: readonly number[], perE: number, allowP = false): number[] {
   205	  return disc.filter((i) => states.every((s) => placeable(s, i, perE, allowP)));
   206	}
   207	
   208	/** Move the whole reserve onto `cells` (even split, remainder to the first): empty cells only, nothing overwritten. */
   209	export function placeInoculum(s: WorldState, a: Audit, cells: readonly number[], words: Uint32Array, allowP = false): TransferRecord {
   210	  return audited("place-inoculum", s, a, () => {
   211	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   212	    const m = cells.length;
   213	    if (m === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("no cell to place the inoculum on");
   214	    const bBase = Math.floor(a.reserveB / m), bRem = a.reserveB % m;
   215	    const eBase = Math.floor(a.reserveE / m), eRem = a.reserveE % m;
   216	    const perE = eBase + (eRem > 0 ? 1 : 0);
   217	    for (const i of cells) if (!placeable(s, i, perE, allowP)) throw new NicheInvalid(`placement refused: cell ${i} is occupied or at its pool cap`);
   218	    const B = a.reserveB, E = a.reserveE;
   219	    cells.forEach((i, j) => {
   220	      s.cells[CH.B * n + i] += bBase + (j < bRem ? 1 : 0);
   221	      s.cells[CH.E * n + i] += eBase + (j < eRem ? 1 : 0);
   222	      for (let g = 0; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) s.genome[g * n + i] = words[g];
   223	    });
   224	    a.reserveB = 0;
   225	    a.reserveE = 0;
   226	    return { placedCells: m, placedB: B, placedE: E };
   227	  });
   228	}
   229	
   230	export const CELLS_LEN = (cfg: WorldConfig): number => cellCount(cfg) * CELL_CHANNELS;
     1	// Arm construction: conditioning histories (builder, nonbuilder), transfers (a co-culture, b builder removed, c matched-inventory
     2	// scramble, cP P-only scramble, cNP non-P scramble, ablation), history-independent controls (d0 receiver alone, d1E energy-matched,
     3	// d1B biomass-matched) and the shared inoculum placement. All arms of a block start at the same absolute step T1 with the same seed.
     4	import { CH, buildWorld, cellCount, encodeGenome, lowbias32, packLineageLo, MOT_ZERO, allocState, type Genome, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     5	import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";
     6	import { PROTOCOL, receiverGenome, worldConfig, type Candidate, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     7	import {
     8	  NicheInvalid,
     9	  assertAudit,
    10	  audited,
    11	  cloneAudit,
    12	  cloneState,
    13	  commonCells,
    14	  convertPolymer,
    15	  fundReserve,
    16	  moveToReserve,
    17	  openAudit,
    18	  placeInoculum,
    19	  relabelLiving,
    20	  scrambleChannels,
    21	  type Audit,
    22	  type TransferRecord,
    23	} from "./transfer.ts";
    24	
    25	export const ARM_IDS = ["a", "b", "c", "cP", "cNP", "nb", "ab", "d0", "d1E", "d1B"] as const;
    26	export type ArmId = (typeof ARM_IDS)[number] | "rb" | "rnb";
    27	
    28	export interface PreArm {
    29	  id: ArmId;
    30	  state: WorldState;
    31	  audit: Audit;
    32	  records: TransferRecord[];
    33	}
    34	
    35	export const disc = (cx: number, cy: number, r: number, W: number, H: number): number[] => {
    36	  const out: number[] = [];
    37	  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) if ((x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2 <= r * r) out.push(y * W + x);
    38	  return out;
    39	};
    40	export const footprintCells = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number[] => disc(p.footprint.x, p.footprint.y, p.footprint.radius, p.grid, p.grid);
    41	/** Footprint plus halo: where inoculum-matching is measured. */
    42	export const regionCells = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number[] => disc(p.footprint.x, p.footprint.y, p.footprint.radius + p.halo, p.grid, p.grid);
    43	export const outsideRegion = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number[] => {
    44	  const inR = new Set(regionCells(p));
    45	  return Array.from({ length: p.grid * p.grid }, (_, i) => i).filter((i) => !inR.has(i));
    46	};
    47	
    48	export function startWorld(cand: Candidate, kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder", seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): WorldState {
    49	  const cfg = worldConfig(seed, p);
    50	  const f = p.founders;
    51	  const s = buildWorld(cfg, { nutrient: p.nutrient, founders: [{ x: f.x, y: f.y, radius: f.radius, genome: cand[kind], biomass: f.biomass, energy: f.energy }] });
    52	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    53	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[CH.E * n + i] += p.backgroundE;
    54	  return s;
    55	}
    56	
    57	export interface History {
    58	  state: WorldState;
    59	  audit: Audit;
    60	  records: TransferRecord[];
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** Stage 1: declared budget, reserve funded at step 0 and inert, T1 steps with the audit checked at every census. */
    64	export function runHistory(cand: Candidate, kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder", seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): History {
    65	  const state = startWorld(cand, kind, seed, p);
    66	  const audit = openAudit(state);
    67	  const records = [fundReserve(state, audit, p.reserve.B, p.reserve.E, outsideRegion(p))];
    68	  const sim = new RefSim(state);
    69	  for (let t = 1; t <= p.T1; t++) {
    70	    sim.step();
    71	    if (t % p.census === 0 || t === p.T1) assertAudit(sim.state, audit, `stage 1 step ${t}`);
    72	  }
    73	  return { state: sim.state, audit, records };
    74	}
    75	
    76	const sumChan = (s: WorldState, chs: readonly number[], idx: readonly number[]): number => {
    77	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    78	  let t = 0;
    79	  for (const ch of chs) for (const i of idx) t += s.cells[ch * n + i];
    80	  return t;
    81	};
    82	
    83	/** Receiver alone: the same total budget as uniform A and uniform E plus the reserve, nothing conditioned, at absolute step T1. */
    84	export interface Budget {
    85	  matterStart: bigint;
    86	  energyStart: bigint;
    87	}
    88	export function receiverAlone(h: { audit: Budget }, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): PreArm {
    89	  const cfg = worldConfig(seed, p);
    90	  const s = allocState(cfg);
    91	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    92	  const a: Audit = { energyStart: h.audit.energyStart, matterStart: h.audit.matterStart, reserveB: p.reserve.B, reserveE: p.reserve.E, heatAnalytic: 0n };
    93	  const spread = (ch: number, total: bigint) => {
    94	    const base = total / BigInt(n), rem = Number(total % BigInt(n));
    95	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.cells[ch * n + i] = Number(base) + (i < rem ? 1 : 0);
    96	  };
    97	  spread(CH.A, a.matterStart - BigInt(p.reserve.B));
    98	  spread(CH.E, a.energyStart - BigInt(cfg.eB) * BigInt(p.reserve.B) - BigInt(p.reserve.E));
    99	  s.cells.fill(MOT_ZERO, CH.MOT * n, (CH.MOT + 1) * n);
   100	  s.step = p.T1;
   101	  assertAudit(s, a, "receiver-alone start");
   102	  return { id: "d0", state: s, audit: a, records: [] };
   103	}
   104	
   105	export interface BlockArms {
   106	  arms: PreArm[];
   107	  histories: { builder: History; nonbuilder: History };
   108	  dE: number;
   109	  dB: number;
   110	}
   111	
   112	/**
   113	 * The "more stored energy / more bound matter explains it" controls: the receiver-alone world with the reserve raised by what the
   114	 * conditioned arm `b` holds in the inoculum footprint plus halo beyond receiver-alone (taken from the lattice outside the region,
   115	 * debits recorded, totals unchanged). d1E raises free energy, d1B raises bound matter with its (eB - eA) chemical-energy debit.
   116	 */
   117	export function matchedControls(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): { d1E: PreArm; d1B: PreArm; dE: number; dB: number } {
   118	  const region = regionCells(p), outside = outsideRegion(p);
   119	  const dE = Math.max(0, sumChan(b.state, [CH.E], region) - sumChan(d0.state, [CH.E], region));
   120	  // Bound matter is B + P. (Summing A and C too would be vacuous: detritus that replaced nutrient on the same cells leaves the total unchanged.)
   121	  const dB = Math.max(0, sumChan(b.state, [CH.B, CH.P], region) - sumChan(d0.state, [CH.B, CH.P], region));
   122	  const d1E: PreArm = { id: "d1E", state: cloneState(d0.state), audit: cloneAudit(d0.audit), records: [] };
   123	  d1E.records.push(audited("matched-energy-debit", d1E.state, d1E.audit, () => (moveToReserve(d1E.state, d1E.audit, 0, dE, outside), { dE })));
   124	  const d1B: PreArm = { id: "d1B", state: cloneState(d0.state), audit: cloneAudit(d0.audit), records: [] };
   125	  d1B.records.push(audited("matched-matter-debit", d1B.state, d1B.audit, () => (moveToReserve(d1B.state, d1B.audit, dB, (d1B.state.cfg.eB - d1B.state.cfg.eA) * dB, outside), { dB })));
   126	  return { d1E, d1B, dE, dB };
   127	}
   128	
   129	/** Build every pre-inoculum arm of one block (nothing placed yet). `replicates` adds the dev-only Stage-2 seed replicates. */
   130	export function buildArms(cand: Candidate, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, replicateSeed?: number): BlockArms {
   131	  const hb = runHistory(cand, "builder", seed, p);
   132	  const hn = runHistory(cand, "nonbuilder", seed, p);
   133	  if (hb.audit.energyStart !== hn.audit.energyStart || hb.audit.matterStart !== hn.audit.matterStart) throw new NicheInvalid("builder and nonbuilder histories start with different budgets");
   134	  const arm = (id: ArmId, h: History, ...ops: ((s: WorldState, a: Audit) => TransferRecord)[]): PreArm => {
   135	    const state = cloneState(h.state), audit = cloneAudit(h.audit);
   136	    return { id, state, audit, records: [...h.records, ...ops.map((f) => f(state, audit))] };
   137	  };
   138	  const mix = lowbias32;
   139	  const bRemoved = (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => relabelLiving(s, a);
   140	  const scr = (chs: number[], op: string) => (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => scrambleChannels(s, a, seed, chs, mix, op);
   141	  const ALL = [CH.A, CH.B, CH.C, CH.P, CH.E, CH.S];
   142	  const arms: PreArm[] = [
   143	    arm("a", hb),
   144	    arm("b", hb, bRemoved),
   145	    arm("c", hb, bRemoved, scr(ALL, "scramble")),
   146	    arm("cP", hb, bRemoved, scr([CH.P], "scramble-P")),
   147	    arm("cNP", hb, bRemoved, scr(ALL.filter((c) => c !== CH.P), "scramble-nonP")),
   148	    arm("nb", hn, bRemoved),
   149	    arm("ab", hb, bRemoved, convertPolymer),
   150	  ];
   151	  const d0 = receiverAlone(hb, seed, p);
   152	  const { d1E, d1B, dE, dB } = matchedControls(arms[1], d0, p);
   153	  arms.push(d0, d1E, d1B);
   154	  if (replicateSeed !== undefined) {
   155	    const rep = (id: ArmId, src: PreArm): PreArm => ({ id, state: cloneState(src.state, { ...src.state.cfg, seed: replicateSeed }), audit: cloneAudit(src.audit), records: [...src.records] });
   156	    arms.push(rep("rb", arms[1]), rep("rnb", arms[5]));
   157	  }
   158	  return { arms, histories: { builder: hb, nonbuilder: hn }, dE, dB };
   159	}
   160	
   161	/** Place the same inoculum on the same cells in every arm: the intersection of placeable footprint cells. Needs at least nMin. */
   162	export function placeAll(arms: PreArm[], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): number[] {
   163	  const cfg = arms[0].state.cfg;
   164	  const maxE = Math.max(...arms.map((x) => x.audit.reserveE));
   165	  const cells = commonCells(arms.map((x) => x.state), footprintCells(p), Math.ceil(maxE / p.nMin), opts.allowP ?? false);
   166	  if (cells.length < p.nMin) throw new NicheInvalid(`only ${cells.length} common placeable cells (< ${p.nMin})`);
   167	  const words = encodeGenome(opts.genome ?? receiverGenome(p), 0, packLineageLo(cfg, p.receiverRaw));
   168	  for (const x of arms) x.records.push(placeInoculum(x.state, x.audit, cells, words, opts.allowP ?? false));
   169	  return cells;
   170	}

    93	/** SHA-256 of the canonical protocol plus the seed-registry digest it was checked against. */

     1	// I06 edge decision: exact integer sign tests, intersection-union within a candidate, Holm across candidates, and the
     2	// three-valued panel verdict. Everything is an integer or a rational {num, den} of integers (no floats), so the sealed
     3	// payload canonicalizes. The rules are those of docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/PLAN.md section 5.3.
     4	import type { EvidenceStatus } from "@bl/schema";
     5	
     6	export interface Rat {
     7	  num: number;
     8	  den: number;
     9	}
    10	
    11	/** a <= b for rationals with positive denominators (all values here are far below 2^53 / each other). */
    12	export const ratLe = (a: Rat, b: Rat): boolean => a.num * b.den <= b.num * a.den;
    13	export const ratMax = (rs: Rat[]): Rat => rs.reduce((m, r) => (ratLe(m, r) ? r : m));
    14	
    15	const choose = (n: number, k: number): number => {
    16	  let c = 1;
    17	  for (let i = 1; i <= k; i++) c = (c * (n - k + i)) / i;
    18	  return Math.round(c);
    19	};
    20	
    21	/** One-sided exact sign test: P(Binomial(n, 1/2) >= k), where k counts strictly positive paired differences (zeros are non-positive). */
    22	export function signTestP(n: number, k: number): Rat {
    23	  let num = 0;
    24	  for (let j = Math.max(0, k); j <= n; j++) num += choose(n, j);
    25	  return { num, den: 2 ** n };
    26	}
    27	
    28	export const positives = (d: readonly number[]): number => d.filter((x) => x > 0).length;
    29	
    30	/** Sum of the two middle values of the sorted differences (n even) or twice the middle one (n odd): median >= delta iff this >= 2*delta. */
    31	export function medianTimes2(d: readonly number[]): number {
    32	  const s = [...d].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    33	  const n = s.length;
    34	  return n % 2 === 1 ? 2 * s[(n - 1) / 2] : s[n / 2 - 1] + s[n / 2];
    35	}
    36	
    37	export interface ContrastResult {
    38	  n: number;
    39	  k: number;
    40	  p: Rat;
    41	  medianTimes2: number;
    42	  /** p <= alpha and median >= delta. */
    43	  passes: boolean;
    44	}
    45	
    46	export function contrastResult(d: readonly number[], delta: number, alpha: Rat): ContrastResult {
    47	  const k = positives(d);
    48	  const p = signTestP(d.length, k);
    49	  const m2 = medianTimes2(d);
    50	  return { n: d.length, k, p, medianTimes2: m2, passes: ratLe(p, alpha) && m2 >= 2 * delta };
    51	}
    52	
    53	export const GATING = ["builder", "poly", "space", "inoc"] as const;
    54	export type Gating = (typeof GATING)[number];
    55	export const ALPHA: Rat = { num: 1, den: 20 };

    57	export interface CandidateInput {
    58	  id: string;
    59	  status: "ok" | "invalid" | "not-run";
    60	  invalidReasons: string[];
    61	  /** Per-block paired differences on the fresh blocks, one array per gating contrast, plus the attribution-only ones. */
    62	  contrasts: Record<Gating, number[]> & { spaceP: number[]; spaceNP: number[] };
    63	  delta: number;
    64	  survivedBlocks: number;
    65	  blocks: number;
    66	  /** The builder history produced polymer on dev (above the replicate floor). */
    67	  polymerAdequate: boolean;
    68	  /** Blocks assigned to the candidate; all must be valid. */
    69	  expectedBlocks: number;
    70	}
    71	
    72	export interface CandidateResult {
    73	  id: string;
    74	  label: EvidenceStatus | "not-run";
    75	  mechanism: "polymer-arrangement-attributed" | "non-polymer field pattern" | "combined conditioned field" | "n/a";
    76	  reasons: string[];
    77	  gating: Record<Gating, ContrastResult>;
    78	  attribution: { spaceP: ContrastResult; spaceNP: ContrastResult };
    79	  pCand: Rat;
    80	  holmThreshold: Rat | null;
    81	  holmRejected: boolean;
    82	  survivalPass: boolean;
    83	}
    84	
    85	export function decideCandidates(cands: readonly CandidateInput[], positiveControlPassed: boolean, minSurvived = 7): CandidateResult[] {
    86	  const ran = cands.filter((c) => c.status !== "not-run");
    87	  const m = ran.length;
    88	  const alphaEach: Rat = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * Math.max(1, m) };
    89	  const results = new Map<string, CandidateResult>();
    90	  const raw = (d: readonly number[], delta: number): ContrastResult => contrastResult(d, delta, { num: 0, den: 1 });
    91	  for (const c of cands) {
    92	    const g = Object.fromEntries(GATING.map((k) => [k, raw(c.contrasts[k], c.delta)])) as Record<Gating, ContrastResult>;
    93	    results.set(c.id, {
    94	      id: c.id,
    95	      label: "not-run",
    96	      mechanism: "n/a",
    97	      reasons: [],
    98	      gating: g,
    99	      attribution: { spaceP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceP, c.delta, alphaEach), spaceNP: contrastResult(c.contrasts.spaceNP, c.delta, alphaEach) },
   100	      pCand: c.status === "ok" && c.blocks === c.expectedBlocks ? ratMax(GATING.map((k) => g[k].p)) : { num: 1, den: 1 },
   101	      holmThreshold: null,
   102	      holmRejected: false,
   103	      survivalPass: c.survivedBlocks >= minSurvived,
   104	    });
   105	  }
   106	  // Holm step-down over the candidates that ran (an invalid one counts with p = 1, never dropped).
   107	  const order = ran.map((c) => results.get(c.id)!).sort((a, b) => a.pCand.num * b.pCand.den - b.pCand.num * a.pCand.den || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1));
   108	  let stopped = false;
   109	  order.forEach((r, i) => {
   110	    r.holmThreshold = { num: ALPHA.num, den: ALPHA.den * (m - i) };
   111	    if (!stopped && ratLe(r.pCand, r.holmThreshold)) r.holmRejected = true;
   112	    else stopped = true;
   113	  });
   114	  // A gating contrast passes when its own p is within the threshold the candidate faced in Holm and its median clears delta.
   115	  for (const c of ran) {
   116	    const r = results.get(c.id)!;
   117	    for (const k of GATING) r.gating[k].passes = ratLe(r.gating[k].p, r.holmThreshold!) && r.gating[k].medianTimes2 >= 2 * c.delta;
   118	  }
   119	  for (const c of ran) {
   120	    const r = results.get(c.id)!;
   121	    if (c.status === "invalid" || c.blocks !== c.expectedBlocks) {
   122	      r.label = "invalid-or-incomplete";
   123	      r.reasons = c.invalidReasons.length ? c.invalidReasons : [`${c.blocks} of ${c.expectedBlocks} blocks valid`];
   124	    } else if (!positiveControlPassed) {
   125	      r.label = "unsupported-measurement";
   126	      r.reasons = ["positive control (hand-wall) shows no spatial dependence in the frozen regime: the assay is insensitive"];
   127	    } else if (!c.polymerAdequate) {
   128	      r.label = "unsupported-measurement";
   129	      r.reasons = ["builder produced no polymer above the replicate floor at the end of conditioning"];
   130	    } else {
   131	      const failed = GATING.filter((k) => !r.gating[k].passes);
   132	      const ok = r.holmRejected && failed.length === 0 && r.survivalPass;
   133	      r.label = ok ? "supported" : "unsupported-within-tested-domain";
   134	      if (!ok) {
   135	        if (failed.length) r.reasons.push(`contrast(s) not passing: ${failed.join(", ")}`);
   136	        if (!r.holmRejected) r.reasons.push("Holm does not reject the candidate");
   137	        if (!r.survivalPass) r.reasons.push("receiver survival rule not met");
   138	      } else {
   139	        r.mechanism = r.attribution.spaceP.passes && !r.attribution.spaceNP.passes ? "polymer-arrangement-attributed" : !r.attribution.spaceP.passes && r.attribution.spaceNP.passes ? "non-polymer field pattern" : "combined conditioned field";
   140	      }
   141	    }
   142	  }
   143	  return cands.map((c) => results.get(c.id)!);
   144	}
   145	
   146	export type PanelVerdict = "causal-benefit-detected" | "no-causal-benefit-detected" | "invalid-or-incomplete" | "unsupported-measurement";
   147	
   148	export interface PanelResult {
   149	  verdict: PanelVerdict;
   150	  text: string;
   151	  supported: string[];
   152	  negative: string[];
   153	  unusable: string[];
   154	}
   155	
   156	export function panelVerdict(results: readonly CandidateResult[]): PanelResult {
   157	  const ran = results.filter((r) => r.label !== "not-run");
   158	  const supported = ran.filter((r) => r.label === "supported").map((r) => r.id);
   159	  const negative = ran.filter((r) => r.label === "unsupported-within-tested-domain").map((r) => r.id);
   160	  const unusable = ran.filter((r) => r.label === "invalid-or-incomplete" || r.label === "unsupported-measurement").map((r) => r.id);
   161	  const notRun = results.filter((r) => r.label === "not-run").map((r) => r.id);
   162	  const tail = `${notRun.length ? ` Not run (runtime rule): ${notRun.join(", ")}.` : ""}`;
   163	  if (supported.length) return { verdict: "causal-benefit-detected", text: `Dependency edge supported for ${supported.join(", ")} within this domain.${unusable.length ? ` Not usable: ${unusable.join(", ")}.` : ""}${tail}`, supported, negative, unusable };
   164	  if (ran.length > 0 && unusable.length === 0) return { verdict: "no-causal-benefit-detected", text: `No causal benefit detected for ${negative.join(", ")} within the tested panel, horizon and field.${tail}`, supported, negative, unusable };
   165	  const anyInvalid = ran.some((r) => r.label === "invalid-or-incomplete");
   166	  return { verdict: anyInvalid ? "invalid-or-incomplete" : "unsupported-measurement", text: `No verdict: usable negatives ${negative.length ? negative.join(", ") : "none"}; not usable ${unusable.join(", ") || "all"}.${tail}`, supported, negative, unusable };
   167	}

   129	/** Build every pre-inoculum arm of one block (nothing placed yet). `replicates` adds the dev-only Stage-2 seed replicates. */
   130	export function buildArms(cand: Candidate, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, replicateSeed?: number): BlockArms {
   131	  const hb = runHistory(cand, "builder", seed, p);
   132	  const hn = runHistory(cand, "nonbuilder", seed, p);
   133	  if (hb.audit.energyStart !== hn.audit.energyStart || hb.audit.matterStart !== hn.audit.matterStart) throw new NicheInvalid("builder and nonbuilder histories start with different budgets");
   134	  const arm = (id: ArmId, h: History, ...ops: ((s: WorldState, a: Audit) => TransferRecord)[]): PreArm => {
   135	    const state = cloneState(h.state), audit = cloneAudit(h.audit);
   136	    return { id, state, audit, records: [...h.records, ...ops.map((f) => f(state, audit))] };
   137	  };
   138	  const mix = lowbias32;
   139	  const bRemoved = (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => relabelLiving(s, a);
   140	  const scr = (chs: number[], op: string) => (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => scrambleChannels(s, a, seed, chs, mix, op);
   141	  const ALL = [CH.A, CH.B, CH.C, CH.P, CH.E, CH.S];
   142	  const arms: PreArm[] = [
   143	    arm("a", hb),
   144	    arm("b", hb, bRemoved),
   145	    arm("c", hb, bRemoved, scr(ALL, "scramble")),
   146	    arm("cP", hb, bRemoved, scr([CH.P], "scramble-P")),
   147	    arm("cNP", hb, bRemoved, scr(ALL.filter((c) => c !== CH.P), "scramble-nonP")),
   148	    arm("nb", hn, bRemoved),
   149	    arm("ab", hb, bRemoved, convertPolymer),
   150	  ];
   151	  const d0 = receiverAlone(hb, seed, p);
   152	  const { d1E, d1B, dE, dB } = matchedControls(arms[1], d0, p);
   153	  arms.push(d0, d1E, d1B);
   154	  if (replicateSeed !== undefined) {
   155	    const rep = (id: ArmId, src: PreArm): PreArm => ({ id, state: cloneState(src.state, { ...src.state.cfg, seed: replicateSeed }), audit: cloneAudit(src.audit), records: [...src.records] });
   156	    arms.push(rep("rb", arms[1]), rep("rnb", arms[5]));
   157	  }
   158	  return { arms, histories: { builder: hb, nonbuilder: hn }, dE, dB };
   159	}
   160	
   161	/** Place the same inoculum on the same cells in every arm: the intersection of placeable footprint cells. Needs at least nMin. */
   162	export function placeAll(arms: PreArm[], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): number[] {
   163	  const cfg = arms[0].state.cfg;
   164	  const maxE = Math.max(...arms.map((x) => x.audit.reserveE));
   165	  const cells = commonCells(arms.map((x) => x.state), footprintCells(p), Math.ceil(maxE / p.nMin), opts.allowP ?? false);
   166	  if (cells.length < p.nMin) throw new NicheInvalid(`only ${cells.length} common placeable cells (< ${p.nMin})`);
   167	  const words = encodeGenome(opts.genome ?? receiverGenome(p), 0, packLineageLo(cfg, p.receiverRaw));
   168	  for (const x of arms) x.records.push(placeInoculum(x.state, x.audit, cells, words, opts.allowP ?? false));
   169	  return cells;
   170	}

     1	// Stage 2 and the readouts. Observers are read-only: they look at sim.state and sim.roles after each step and never write.
     2	import { CH, G, cellCount, packLineageLo, stateHash, totalsOf, validateState, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     3	import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";
     4	import { buildArms, placeAll, type ArmId, type BlockArms, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     5	import { PROTOCOL, candidateOf, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     6	import { NicheInvalid, assertAudit, snap, type Snap, type TransferRecord } from "./transfer.ts";
     7	
     8	export interface ArmOutcome {
     9	  id: ArmId;
    10	  /** Receiver-labelled biomass inventory B at the start of Stage 2 and at each census (last entry is T2). */
    11	  receiverB: number[];
    12	  /** Receiver-labelled B at T2: the primary endpoint Y. */
    13	  y: number;
    14	  survived: boolean;
    15	  builderB: number;
    16	  /** Receiver-cell PHOTO + GROW quanta summed over every Stage 2 step (synthesis), and DECOMP, RESP. */
    17	  synthesis: number;
    18	  decomp: number;
    19	  resp: number;
    20	  /** A role counter reached its saturation value at some step: flux attribution is unavailable. */
    21	  saturated: boolean;
    22	  /** Y - B_r gain is at least half synthesis: "growth" may be said. Otherwise "inventory benefit, growth not attributed". */
    23	  growthAttributed: boolean;
    24	  energyEnd: string;
    25	  endHash: string;
    26	}
    27	
    28	export interface Hooks {
    29	  /** Called after each step with the state and the roles buffer (read only). */
    30	  onStep?: (state: WorldState, roles: Uint32Array, step: number) => void;
    31	}
    32	
    33	const lin = (s: WorldState, raw: number) => packLineageLo(s.cfg, raw);
    34	
    35	function receiverB(s: WorldState, raw: number): number {
    36	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    37	  const lo = lin(s, raw);
    38	  let t = 0;
    39	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0 && s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] === lo) t += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    40	  return t;
    41	}
    42	
    43	/** Run Stage 2 on a placed arm for T2 steps; throws NicheInvalid on any residual. */
    44	export function runArm(x: PreArm, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, hooks: Hooks = {}): ArmOutcome {
    45	  const sim = new RefSim(x.state);
    46	  const n = cellCount(x.state.cfg);
    47	  const lo = lin(x.state, p.receiverRaw), blo = lin(x.state, p.builderRaw);
    48	  const y0 = receiverB(sim.state, p.receiverRaw);
    49	  const series = [y0];
    50	  let synthesis = 0, decomp = 0, resp = 0, saturated = false;
    51	  for (let t = 1; t <= p.T2; t++) {
    52	    sim.step();
    53	    const s = sim.state, roles = sim.roles;
    54	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    55	      const w0 = roles[2 * i], w1 = roles[2 * i + 1];
    56	      if ((w0 & 0xffff) >= p.roleSaturation || w0 >>> 16 >= p.roleSaturation || (w1 & 0xffff) >= p.roleSaturation || w1 >>> 16 >= p.roleSaturation) saturated = true;
    57	      // Roles are counted on every cell where the builder was removed (only receivers react there; injury may clear a lineage after it synthesized). Co-culture counts by lineage and is approximate under injury.
    58	      if (x.id !== "a" || (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0 && s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] === lo)) {
    59	        synthesis += (w0 & 0xffff) + (w0 >>> 16);
    60	        decomp += w1 & 0xffff;
    61	        resp += w1 >>> 16;
    62	      }
    63	    }
    64	    hooks.onStep?.(s, roles, s.step);
    65	    if (t % p.census === 0 || t === p.T2) {
    66	      assertAudit(s, x.audit, `stage 2 ${x.id} step ${t}`);
    67	      series.push(receiverB(s, p.receiverRaw));
    68	    }
    69	  }
    70	  const s = sim.state;
    71	  const errs = validateState(s);
    72	  if (errs.length) throw new NicheInvalid(`stage 2 ${x.id}: ${errs[0]}`);
    73	  let builderB = 0;
    74	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0 && s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] === blo) builderB += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    75	  const y = series[series.length - 1];
    76	  const gain = y - y0;
    77	  return {
    78	    id: x.id,
    79	    receiverB: series,
    80	    y,
    81	    survived: y > 0,
    82	    builderB,
    83	    synthesis,
    84	    decomp,
    85	    resp,
    86	    saturated,
    87	    growthAttributed: !saturated && gain > 0 && 2 * synthesis >= gain,
    88	    energyEnd: `${totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells).energy}`,
    89	    endHash: stateHash(s),
    90	  };
    91	}

    93	export interface HistoryCost {
    94	  kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder";
    95	  B: string;
    96	  P: string;
    97	  E: string;
    98	  A: string;
    99	  C: string;
   100	  lightIn: string;
   101	  heatOut: string;
   102	  builderLineageB: number;
   103	  living: number;
   104	  /** kGene maintenance is not separable from catalytic and ordinary maintenance in react(); stated, not inferred. */
   105	  kGeneMaintenance: "unavailable";
   106	}
   107	
   108	export function historyCost(kind: "builder" | "nonbuilder", s: WorldState, p: Protocol): HistoryCost {
   109	  const t = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
   110	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   111	  let b = 0, living = 0;
   112	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   113	    const hi = s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i], lo = s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i];
   114	    if ((hi | lo) !== 0) living++;
   115	    if (hi === 0 && lo === lin(s, p.builderRaw)) b += s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
   116	  }
   117	  return { kind, B: `${t.B}`, P: `${t.P}`, E: `${t.E}`, A: `${t.A}`, C: `${t.C}`, lightIn: `${s.lightIn}`, heatOut: `${s.heatOut}`, builderLineageB: b, living, kGeneMaintenance: "unavailable" };
   118	}

   120	export interface BlockResult {
   121	  seed: number;
   122	  cluster: number;
   123	  valid: boolean;
   124	  invalidReason: string | null;
   125	  nPlaced: number;
   126	  dE: number;
   127	  dB: number;
   128	  costs: HistoryCost[];
   129	  startTotals: Record<string, Snap>;
   130	  transfers: Record<string, TransferRecord[]>;
   131	  outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>;
   132	  /** Integer contrasts (null when invalid). */
   133	  contrasts: Contrasts | null;
   134	}
   135	
   136	export interface Contrasts {
   137	  builder: number;
   138	  poly: number;
   139	  space: number;
   140	  inoc: number;
   141	  spaceP: number;
   142	  spaceNP: number;
   143	  naive: number;
   144	  /** Stage-2 replicate differences (dev only). */
   145	  replicateBuilder: number | null;
   146	  replicateNonbuilder: number | null;
   147	}
   148	
   149	export function contrastsOf(o: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>): Contrasts {
   150	  const y = (id: ArmId): number => {
   151	    const v = o[id];
   152	    if (!v) throw new NicheInvalid(`arm ${id} missing`);
   153	    return v.y;
   154	  };
   155	  const yb = y("b");
   156	  return {
   157	    builder: yb - y("nb"),
   158	    poly: yb - y("ab"),
   159	    space: yb - y("c"),
   160	    inoc: yb - Math.max(y("d1E"), y("d1B")),
   161	    spaceP: yb - y("cP"),
   162	    spaceNP: yb - y("cNP"),
   163	    naive: yb - y("d0"),
   164	    replicateBuilder: o.rb ? yb - o.rb.y : null,
   165	    replicateNonbuilder: o.rnb ? y("nb") - o.rnb.y : null,
   166	  };
   167	}
   168	
   169	/** One block: Stage 1 histories, transfers, shared placement, all Stage 2 arms. An invalid block is reported, never dropped. */
   170	export function runBlock(cluster: number, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, replicateSeed?: number, hooks: Hooks = {}): BlockResult {
   171	  const base: BlockResult = { seed, cluster, valid: false, invalidReason: null, nPlaced: 0, dE: 0, dB: 0, costs: [], startTotals: {}, transfers: {}, outcomes: {}, contrasts: null };
   172	  let built: BlockArms | null = null;
   173	  try {
   174	    built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p, replicateSeed);
   175	    base.dE = built.dE;
   176	    base.dB = built.dB;
   177	    base.costs = [historyCost("builder", built.histories.builder.state, p), historyCost("nonbuilder", built.histories.nonbuilder.state, p)];
   178	    base.nPlaced = placeAll(built.arms, p).length;
   179	    for (const x of built.arms) {
   180	      base.startTotals[x.id] = snap(x.state, x.audit);
   181	      base.transfers[x.id] = x.records;
   182	    }
   183	    for (const x of built.arms) base.outcomes[x.id] = runArm(x, p, hooks);
   184	    base.contrasts = contrastsOf(base.outcomes);
   185	    base.valid = true;
   186	  } catch (e) {
   187	    if (!(e instanceof NicheInvalid)) throw e;
   188	    base.invalidReason = e.reason;
   189	    if (built) for (const x of built.arms) base.transfers[x.id] ??= x.records;
   190	  }
   191	  return base;
   192	}

     1	// Fixtures: the negative case that fools the naive metric (energy-gift), the positive control (hand-wall), the relabelling trap
     2	// (takeover-only), a null field, and the guard checks. Unit-scale fixtures use 16x16 worlds with kernelRadius 5 (schema validation
     3	// needs dimensions divisible by eight and 2*kernelRadius+1 <= tile); the positive control is also qualified at the frozen scale.
     4	import { CH, cellCount, encodeGenome, founderGenome, lowbias32, packLineageLo, type Genome, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     5	import { disc, footprintCells, matchedControls, outsideRegion, placeAll, receiverAlone, regionCells, startWorld, type ArmId, type Budget, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     6	import { contrastsOf, runArm, type ArmOutcome, type Contrasts } from "./assay.ts";
     7	import { decideCandidates, type CandidateResult } from "./decision.ts";
     8	import { PROTOCOL, candidateOf, founderOf, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     9	import { NicheInvalid, audited, cloneAudit, cloneState, convertPolymer, debitEven, openAudit, placeInoculum, scrambleChannels, type Audit } from "./transfer.ts";

    11	export const FIXTURE_SEEDS: readonly number[] = [8_600_401, 8_600_402, 8_600_403, 8_600_404, 8_600_405, 8_600_406, 8_600_407, 8_600_408];

    13	export function fixtureProtocol(): Protocol {
    14	  return { ...PROTOCOL, grid: 16, T1: 0, T2: 40, injury: null, footprint: { x: 8, y: 8, radius: 2 }, halo: 2, nMin: 8, reserve: { B: 480, E: 24 }, nutrient: 100 };
    15	}
    16	
    17	/** Uniform-A / uniform-E budget of a w x w lattice with the reserve inside it (no founders). */
    18	export const uniformBudget = (p: Protocol): Budget => {
    19	  const n = p.grid * p.grid;
    20	  return { matterStart: BigInt(p.nutrient * n), energyStart: BigInt(p.backgroundE * n + 10 * p.reserve.B + p.reserve.E) };
    21	};
    22	
    23	const setChannel = (s: WorldState, ch: number, cells: readonly number[], total: number): void => {
    24	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), m = cells.length, base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
    25	  cells.forEach((i, j) => (s.cells[ch * n + i] += base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0)));
    26	};
    27	
    28	/** Run all pre-inoculum arms of a fixture block through the shared placement and Stage 2. */
    29	export function runPre(arms: PreArm[], p: Protocol, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>> {
    30	  placeAll(arms, p, opts);
    31	  const out: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>> = {};
    32	  for (const x of arms) out[x.id] = runArm(x, p);
    33	  return out;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** Controls around a conditioned pre-arm `b` (any hand-built field): scrambles, ablation, receiver-alone and matched-inventory. */
    37	export function controlsAround(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: Protocol): PreArm[] {
    38	  const mk = (id: ArmId, ...ops: ((s: WorldState, a: Audit) => unknown)[]): PreArm => {
    39	    const state = cloneState(b.state), audit = cloneAudit(b.audit);
    40	    ops.forEach((f) => f(state, audit));
    41	    return { id, state, audit, records: [] };
    42	  };
    43	  const ALL = [CH.A, CH.B, CH.C, CH.P, CH.E, CH.S];
    44	  const sc = (chs: number[]) => (s: WorldState, a: Audit) => scrambleChannels(s, a, seed, chs, lowbias32);
    45	  const { d1E, d1B } = matchedControls(b, d0, p);
    46	  const nb = { id: "nb" as ArmId, state: cloneState(d0.state), audit: cloneAudit(d0.audit), records: [] };
    47	  return [
    48	    { ...b, id: "b" },
    49	    mk("c", sc(ALL)),
    50	    mk("cP", sc([CH.P])),
    51	    mk("cNP", sc(ALL.filter((c) => c !== CH.P))),
    52	    mk("ab", (s, a) => convertPolymer(s, a)),
    53	    nb,
    54	    d0,
    55	    d1E,
    56	    d1B,
    57	  ];
    58	}
    59	
    60	/** Halo ring cells: in the footprint-plus-halo region but not in the footprint. */
    61	export const regionCellsExcludingFootprint = (p: Protocol): number[] => {
    62	  const fp = new Set(footprintCells(p));
    63	  return regionCells(p).filter((i) => !fp.has(i));
    64	};
    65	
    66	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    67	// energy-gift
    68	
    69	export type GiftVariant = "uniform" | "footprint" | "annulus";
    70	/** Extra stored free energy per variant, sized so the naive metric clears the fixture delta in each. */
    71	export const GIFT: Record<GiftVariant, number> = { uniform: 10000, footprint: 4000, annulus: 4000 };
    72	
    73	/** A world that differs from receiver-alone only by extra stored free energy, declared in its budget (it is a different budget on purpose). */
    74	export function giftBlock(variant: GiftVariant, seed: number, p: Protocol = fixtureProtocol(), gift = GIFT[variant]): { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts; gift: number } {
    75	  const cfgSeed = seed;
    76	  const budget = uniformBudget(p);
    77	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: budget }, cfgSeed, p);
    78	  const cond: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
    79	  const W = p.grid;
    80	  const cells = variant === "uniform" ? Array.from({ length: W * W }, (_, i) => i) : variant === "footprint" ? footprintCells(p) : regionCellsExcludingFootprint(p);
    81	  setChannel(cond.state, CH.E, cells, gift);
    82	  cond.audit.energyStart += BigInt(gift);
    83	  const arms = controlsAround(cond, base, seed, p);
    84	  const outcomes = runPre(arms, p);
    85	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOf(outcomes), gift };
    86	}
    87	
    88	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89	// hand-wall
    90	
    91	export type WallKind = "W1" | "W2";
    92	
    93	/** W1: a polymer ring around the footprint (gates A/C diffusion). W2: a polymer floor under the footprint cells (a local-P shield under injuryShell). */
    94	export function wallCells(kind: WallKind, p: Protocol): number[] {
    95	  const f = p.footprint;
    96	  if (kind === "W2") return footprintCells(p);
    97	  const inner = new Set(disc(f.x, f.y, f.radius + p.wall.ringGap - 1, p.grid, p.grid));
    98	  return disc(f.x, f.y, f.radius + p.wall.ringGap + 1, p.grid, p.grid).filter((i) => !inner.has(i));
    99	}
   100	
   101	/** Build P on `cells` out of lattice nutrient on the same cells and pay (eP - eA) * P from lattice E outside the region. */
   102	export function buildWall(x: PreArm, cells: readonly number[], totalP: number, p: Protocol): void {
   103	  audited("build-wall", x.state, x.audit, () => {
   104	    const s = x.state;
   105	    debitEven(s, CH.A, totalP, cells);
   106	    setChannel(s, CH.P, cells, totalP);
   107	    debitEven(s, CH.E, (s.cfg.eP - s.cfg.eA) * totalP, outsideRegion(p));
   108	    return { totalP, cells: cells.length };
   109	  });
   110	}
   111	
   112	/** One hand-wall block: the wall against the same P inventory scrambled. D = Y(wall) - Y(P scrambled). */
   113	export function wallBlock(kind: WallKind, seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, budget?: Budget): { d: number; wall: ArmOutcome; scrambled: ArmOutcome } {
   114	  const bud = budget ?? nominalBudget(seed, p);
   115	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: bud }, seed, p);
   116	  const wall: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
   117	  buildWall(wall, wallCells(kind, p), p.wall.totalP, p);
   118	  const scr: PreArm = { id: "cP", state: cloneState(wall.state), audit: cloneAudit(wall.audit), records: [] };
   119	  scrambleChannels(scr.state, scr.audit, seed, [CH.P], lowbias32, "scramble-P");
   120	  const out = runPre([wall, scr], p, { allowP: true });
   121	  return { d: out.b!.y - out.cP!.y, wall: out.b!, scrambled: out.cP! };
   122	}
   123	
   124	/** The declared initial budget of a real block at this seed (founders included), without running anything. */
   125	export function nominalBudget(seed: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Budget {
   126	  const a = openAudit(startWorld(candidateOf(p.panel[0], p), "builder", seed, p));
   127	  return { matterStart: a.matterStart, energyStart: a.energyStart };
   128	}
   129	
   130	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   131	// takeover-only
   132	
   133	/** A receiver that cannot synthesize anything: zero controller weights, no gene bits. */
   134	export const inertReceiver = (p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Genome => ({ ...founderGenome(founderOf(p.receiverCluster)), weights: new Int8Array(160), genes: 0 });
   135	
   136	/** Non-living detritus B on the halo ring next to the footprint (funded from A there and from E elsewhere); an inert receiver is placed. */
   137	export function takeoverBlock(seed: number, p: Protocol = fixtureProtocol(), detritus = 200): { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts } {
   138	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
   139	  const cond: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
   140	  const ring = regionCellsExcludingFootprint(p);
   141	  audited("detritus", cond.state, cond.audit, () => {
   142	    const s = cond.state;
   143	    debitEven(s, CH.A, detritus, ring);
   144	    setChannel(s, CH.B, ring, detritus);
   145	    debitEven(s, CH.E, (s.cfg.eB - s.cfg.eA) * detritus, outsideRegion(p));
   146	    return { detritus };
   147	  });
   148	  const outcomes = runPre(controlsAround(cond, base, seed, p), p, { genome: inertReceiver(p) });
   149	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOf(outcomes) };
   150	}
   151	
   152	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   153	// null field and guards
   154	
   155	export function nullBlock(seed: number, p: Protocol = fixtureProtocol()): { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts } {
   156	  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
   157	  const cond: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
   158	  const outcomes = runPre(controlsAround(cond, base, seed, p), p);
   159	  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOf(outcomes) };
   160	}
   161	
   162	export interface GuardResult {
   163	  id: string;
   164	  ok: boolean;
   165	  detail: string;
   166	}
   167	
   168	/** Guards that must refuse or flag: each returns ok when the bad input was refused as designed. */
   169	export function runGuards(): GuardResult[] {
   170	  const p = fixtureProtocol();
   171	  const out: GuardResult[] = [];
   172	  const attempt = (id: string, fn: () => void, want: RegExp): void => {
   173	    try {
   174	      fn();
   175	      out.push({ id, ok: false, detail: "no refusal" });
   176	    } catch (e) {
   177	      const ok = e instanceof NicheInvalid && want.test(e.reason);
   178	      out.push({ id, ok, detail: e instanceof NicheInvalid ? e.reason : String(e) });
   179	    }
   180	  };
   181	  const fresh = () => receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, 1, p);
   182	  const words = encodeGenome(founderGenome(founderOf(p.receiverCluster)), 0, packLineageLo(fresh().state.cfg, p.receiverRaw));
   183	  attempt("overwrite-attempt", () => {
   184	    const x = fresh();
   185	    x.state.cells[CH.B * cellCount(x.state.cfg) + footprintCells(p)[0]] = 5;
   186	    x.audit.energyStart += 50n;
   187	    x.audit.matterStart += 5n;
   188	    placeInoculum(x.state, x.audit, footprintCells(p), words);
   189	  }, /refused/);
   190	  attempt("pool-cap", () => {
   191	    const x = fresh();
   192	    const i = footprintCells(p)[0];
   193	    const old = x.state.cells[CH.E * cellCount(x.state.cfg) + i];
   194	    x.state.cells[CH.E * cellCount(x.state.cfg) + i] = 2 ** 28 - 1;
   195	    x.audit.energyStart += BigInt(2 ** 28 - 1 - old);
   196	    placeInoculum(x.state, x.audit, footprintCells(p), words);
   197	  }, /refused/);
   198	  attempt("underfunded-reserve", () => {
   199	    const x = fresh();
   200	    debitEven(x.state, CH.A, 10 ** 9, [0]);
   201	  }, /underfunded/);
   202	  attempt("empty-receiver-reserve", () => {
   203	    const x = fresh();
   204	    placeInoculum(x.state, x.audit, footprintCells(p), words);
   205	    placeInoculum(x.state, cloneAudit(x.audit), footprintCells(p), words);
   206	  }, /refused|no cell|occupied/);
   207	  return out;
   208	}

   210	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   211	// fixture decisions
   212	
   213	export interface FixtureReport {
   214	  id: string;
   215	  seeds: readonly number[];
   216	  contrasts: Contrasts[];
   217	  naiveK: number;
   218	  naiveWouldSay: "benefit" | "no benefit";
   219	  matchedControls: "caught" | "not caught" | "n/a";
   220	  label: CandidateResult["label"];
   221	  growthAttributed: boolean[];
   222	  synthesis: number[];
   223	}
   224	
   225	const col = (cs: Contrasts[], k: keyof Contrasts): number[] => cs.map((c) => c[k] as number);
   226	
   227	export function decideFixture(id: string, seeds: readonly number[], results: { outcomes: Partial<Record<ArmId, ArmOutcome>>; contrasts: Contrasts }[], delta: number): FixtureReport {
   228	  const cs = results.map((r) => r.contrasts);
   229	  const dec = decideCandidates(
   230	    [
   231	      {
   232	        id,
   233	        status: "ok",
   234	        invalidReasons: [],
   235	        contrasts: { builder: col(cs, "builder"), poly: col(cs, "poly"), space: col(cs, "space"), inoc: col(cs, "inoc"), spaceP: col(cs, "spaceP"), spaceNP: col(cs, "spaceNP") },
   236	        delta,
   237	        survivedBlocks: results.filter((r) => r.outcomes.b?.survived).length,
   238	        blocks: results.length,
   239	        polymerAdequate: true,
   240	        expectedBlocks: results.length,
   241	      },
   242	    ],
   243	    true,
   244	  )[0];
   245	  const naiveK = col(cs, "naive").filter((x) => x > 0).length;
   246	  const naivePositive = naiveK * 2 > results.length && col(cs, "naive").sort((a, b) => a - b)[Math.floor(results.length / 2)] >= delta;
   247	  return {
   248	    id,
   249	    seeds,
   250	    contrasts: cs,
   251	    naiveK,
   252	    naiveWouldSay: naivePositive ? "benefit" : "no benefit",
   253	    matchedControls: naivePositive ? (dec.label === "supported" ? "not caught" : "caught") : "n/a",
   254	    label: dec.label,
   255	    growthAttributed: results.map((r) => !!r.outcomes.b?.growthAttributed),
   256	    synthesis: results.map((r) => r.outcomes.b?.synthesis ?? 0),
   257	  };
   258	}

    10	describe("niche fixtures", () => {
    11	  for (const v of ["uniform", "footprint", "annulus"] as const) {
    12	    it(`energy-gift ${v}: naive metric positive, edge unsupported, and the matched control that catches it`, () => {
    13	      const rs = FIXTURE_SEEDS.map((s) => giftBlock(v, s, fp));
    14	      const rep = decideFixture(`gift-${v}`, FIXTURE_SEEDS, rs, delta);
    15	      expect(rep.naiveWouldSay).toBe("benefit");
    16	      expect(rep.naiveK).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(7);
    17	      expect(rep.label).toBe("unsupported-within-tested-domain");
    18	      expect(rep.matchedControls).toBe("caught");
    19	      const kOf = (key: "space" | "inoc") => rs.filter((r) => r.contrasts[key] > 0).length;
    20	      const notSignificant = (k: number) => signTestP(8, k).num * 20 > signTestP(8, k).den;
    21	      // uniform gift: the scramble leaves it unchanged, so it cannot look spatial. Footprint and annulus gifts: the scramble would mislabel them spatial, the energy-matched inoculum does not.
    22	      if (v === "uniform") expect(notSignificant(kOf("space"))).toBe(true);
    23	      else expect(notSignificant(kOf("inoc"))).toBe(true);

    19	describe("niche assay", () => {
    20	  it("instrumented twin equals plain twin (stateHash): the readout does not change physics", () => {
    21	    const b = placed();
    22	    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    23	    const plain = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state));
    24	    plain.run(short.T2);
    25	    let calls = 0;
    26	    const out = runArm({ ...x, state: cloneState(x.state), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit) }, short, { onStep: () => void calls++ });
    27	    expect(out.endHash).toBe(stateHash(plain.state));
    28	    expect(calls).toBe(short.T2);
    29	  });
    30	
    31	  it("ledgerResidual is 0n at every census of both stages (census every step)", () => {
    32	    const r = runBlock(0, seed, short);
    33	    expect(r.valid, r.invalidReason ?? "").toBe(true);
    34	    for (const o of Object.values(r.outcomes)) expect(o!.receiverB.length).toBe(short.T2 + 1);
    35	  });
    36	
    37	  it("deterministic replay: same block twice gives equal payload bytes", () => {
    38	    const a = canonicalJSON(compactBlock(runBlock(0, seed, short, 77)));
    39	    const b = canonicalJSON(compactBlock(runBlock(0, seed, short, 77)));
    40	    expect(a).toBe(b);
    41	  });
    42	
    43	  it("segmented stage 2 (checkpoint round trip at the midpoint) equals continuous", () => {
    44	    const b = placed();
    45	    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    46	    const cont = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state));
    47	    cont.run(short.T2);
    48	    const half = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state));
    49	    half.run(short.T2 / 2);
    50	    const back = decodeCheckpoint(encodeCheckpoint(half.state)).state;
    51	    const rest = new RefSim(back);
    52	    rest.run(short.T2 / 2);
    53	    expect(stateHash(rest.state)).toBe(stateHash(cont.state));
    54	  });
    55	
    56	  it("mutation off: no new lineage ids", () => {
    57	    const b = placed();
    58	    const seen = new Set<string>();
    59	    for (const x of b.arms) {
    60	      runArm(x, short, {
    61	        onStep: (s) => {
    62	          const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    63	          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if ((s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) !== 0) seen.add(`${s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i]}:${s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]}`);
    64	        },
    65	      });
    66	    }
    67	    expect([...seen].sort()).toEqual(["0:1", "0:2"]);
    68	  });
    69	
    70	  it("flux attribution unavailable when a role counter reaches saturation", () => {
    71	    const b = placed();
    72	    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    73	    const sat = runArm({ ...x, state: cloneState(x.state), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit) }, { ...short, roleSaturation: 1 });
    74	    expect(sat.saturated).toBe(true);
    75	    expect(sat.growthAttributed).toBe(false);
    76	    const ok = runArm({ ...x, state: cloneState(x.state), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit) }, short);
    77	    expect(ok.saturated).toBe(false);
    78	  });
    79	
    80	  it("observer reads roles every Stage 2 step", () => {
    81	    const b = placed();
    82	    const steps: number[] = [];
    83	    runArm(b.arms[0], short, { onStep: (s, roles) => (roles.length > 0 ? steps.push(s.step) : undefined) });
    84	    expect(steps).toEqual(Array.from({ length: short.T2 }, (_, i) => short.T1 + i + 1));
    85	  });
    86	
    87	  it("a failed positive control runs no fresh seed and the panel is unsupported-measurement, and it still seals", () => {
    88	    const dev = { wall: { positiveControlPassed: false } } as unknown as DevReport;
    89	    const f = freshReport(dev, PROTOCOL);
    90	    expect(f.ran).toBe(false);
    91	    expect(f.candidates).toEqual([]);
    92	    expect(f.panel.verdict).toBe("unsupported-measurement");
    93	    expect(() => canonicalJSON(f)).not.toThrow();
    94	  });
    95	});

     1	// Evidence assembly: dev (calibration) report, fresh (confirmation) report, fixtures, guards, the edge decision, and the seal.
     2	// Pure and deterministic: no timestamps, no timings. Integers, integer rationals and decimal strings only.
     3	import { canonicalJSON, sha256HexJS } from "@bl/schema";
     4	import type { ArmId } from "./arms.ts";
     5	import { runBlock, type ArmOutcome, type BlockResult, type Contrasts } from "./assay.ts";
     6	import { ALPHA, decideCandidates, contrastResult, panelVerdict, type CandidateInput, type CandidateResult, type ContrastResult, type PanelResult } from "./decision.ts";
     7	import { GIFT, FIXTURE_SEEDS, decideFixture, fixtureProtocol, giftBlock, nullBlock, runGuards, takeoverBlock, wallBlock, type FixtureReport, type GiftVariant, type GuardResult, type WallKind } from "./fixtures.ts";

    10	export const EVIDENCE_VERSION = "niche-evidence-v1";
    11	
    12	const OUT_FIELDS = ["y", "receiverB", "survived", "builderB", "synthesis", "decomp", "resp", "saturated", "growthAttributed", "energyEnd", "endHash"] as const;
    13	
    14	export interface CompactBlock {
    15	  seed: number;
    16	  cluster: number;
    17	  valid: boolean;
    18	  invalidReason: string | null;
    19	  nPlaced: number;
    20	  dE: number;
    21	  dB: number;
    22	  costs: BlockResult["costs"];
    23	  startTotals: BlockResult["startTotals"];
    24	  /** Per arm: each transfer op with its exact residuals. */
    25	  transfers: Record<string, { op: string; residual: string; matterResidual: string; detail: Record<string, number | string> }[]>;
    26	  /** The complete before/after inventories (funding, removal, placement, matched-control debits) of the builder-removed arm and the two matched controls. */
    27	  inventories: Record<string, BlockResult["transfers"][string]>;
    28	  outcomes: Record<string, Pick<ArmOutcome, (typeof OUT_FIELDS)[number]>>;
    29	  contrasts: Contrasts | null;
    30	}
    31	
    32	export function compactBlock(b: BlockResult): CompactBlock {
    33	  return {
    34	    seed: b.seed,
    35	    cluster: b.cluster,
    36	    valid: b.valid,
    37	    invalidReason: b.invalidReason,
    38	    nPlaced: b.nPlaced,
    39	    dE: b.dE,
    40	    dB: b.dB,
    41	    costs: b.costs,
    42	    startTotals: b.startTotals,
    43	    transfers: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(b.transfers).map(([k, v]) => [k, v.map((r) => ({ op: r.op, residual: r.residual, matterResidual: r.matterResidual, detail: r.detail }))])),
    44	    inventories: Object.fromEntries((['b', 'd1E', 'd1B'] as const).map((k) => [k, b.transfers[k] ?? []])),
    45	    outcomes: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(b.outcomes).map(([k, v]) => [k, Object.fromEntries(OUT_FIELDS.map((f) => [f, (v as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[f]]))])) as CompactBlock["outcomes"],
    46	    contrasts: b.contrasts,
    47	  };
    48	}
    49	
    50	const median = (xs: readonly number[]): number => {
    51	  const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    52	  return s[Math.floor(s.length / 2)];
    53	};
    54	const medianOrNull = (xs: readonly number[]): number | null => (xs.length ? median(xs) : null);
    55	
    56	export interface WallReport {
    57	  kind: WallKind;
    58	  totalP: number;
    59	  seeds: readonly number[];
    60	  d: number[];
    61	  k: number;
    62	  p: { num: number; den: number };
    63	  medianTimes2: number;
    64	  passes: boolean;
    65	  wallY: number[];
    66	  scrambledY: number[];
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function wallReport(kind: WallKind, totalP: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): WallReport {
    70	  const pp: Protocol = { ...p, wall: { ...p.wall, totalP } };
    71	  const runs = p.wallSeeds.map((s) => wallBlock(kind, s, pp));
    72	  const delta = Math.ceil(p.reserve.B / 50);
    73	  const r: ContrastResult = contrastResult(runs.map((x) => x.d), delta, ALPHA);
    74	  return { kind, totalP, seeds: p.wallSeeds, d: runs.map((x) => x.d), k: r.k, p: r.p, medianTimes2: r.medianTimes2, passes: r.passes, wallY: runs.map((x) => x.wall.y), scrambledY: runs.map((x) => x.scrambled.y) };
    75	}
    76	
    77	export interface DevCandidate {
    78	  cluster: number;
    79	  blocks: CompactBlock[];
    80	  /** |Y(b|X, seed) - Y(b|X, replicate seed)| for X in builder, nonbuilder, over the dev blocks. */
    81	  replicateAbs: number[];
    82	  deltaProposed: number;
    83	  builderPMedian: number;
    84	  polymerAdequate: boolean;
    85	}
    86	
    87	export interface DevReport {
    88	  purpose: "calibration";
    89	  seeds: readonly number[];
    90	  candidates: DevCandidate[];
    91	  wall: { W1: WallReport; W2: WallReport; builderScale: { W1: WallReport; W2: WallReport }; positiveControlPassed: boolean; used: WallKind | null };
    92	}
    93	
    94	export function deltaOf(reserveB: number, replicateAbs: readonly number[]): number {
    95	  const m = replicateAbs.length ? Math.max(...replicateAbs) : 0;
    96	  return Math.max(Math.ceil(reserveB / 50), Math.ceil((3 * m) / 2));
    97	}
    98	
    99	export function devReport(p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): DevReport {
   100	  const candidates: DevCandidate[] = p.panel.map((cluster) => {
   101	    const blocks = p.devSeeds.map((s, i) => compactBlock(runBlock(cluster, s, p, p.devReplicateSeeds[i])));
   102	    const rep = blocks.flatMap((b) => (b.contrasts ? [Math.abs(b.contrasts.replicateBuilder ?? 0), Math.abs(b.contrasts.replicateNonbuilder ?? 0)] : []));
   103	    const pMed = median(blocks.map((b) => Number(b.costs.find((c) => c.kind === "builder")?.P ?? 0)));
   104	    return { cluster, blocks, replicateAbs: rep, deltaProposed: deltaOf(p.reserve.B, rep), builderPMedian: pMed, polymerAdequate: pMed > p.polymerFloor };
   105	  });
   106	  const W1 = wallReport("W1", p.wall.totalP, p), W2 = wallReport("W2", p.wall.totalP, p);
   107	  const bs = { W1: wallReport("W1", p.wall.builderScaleP, p), W2: wallReport("W2", p.wall.builderScaleP, p) };
   108	  return { purpose: "calibration", seeds: p.devSeeds, candidates, wall: { W1, W2, builderScale: bs, positiveControlPassed: W1.passes || W2.passes, used: W2.passes ? "W2" : W1.passes ? "W1" : null } };
   109	}
   110	
   111	export interface FreshCandidate {
   112	  cluster: number;
   113	  blocks: CompactBlock[];
   114	  input: CandidateInput;
   115	  /** Per-arm median receiver-labelled biomass at T2 over the fresh blocks (integer; the lower middle value). */
   116	  medianY: Record<string, number | null>;
   117	  fluxAttribution: { available: boolean; reason: string; growthAttributedBlocks: number };
   118	}
   119	
   120	export interface FreshReport {
   121	  purpose: "confirmation";
   122	  seeds: readonly number[];
   123	  candidates: FreshCandidate[];
   124	  results: CandidateResult[];
   125	  panel: PanelResult;
   126	  /** false when the positive control failed on dev: no confirmation seed was run (PLAN gate G3). */
   127	  ran: boolean;
   128	  /** Stated caveats that qualify how to read the panel verdict (sensitivity at builder-scale polymer, replicate noise). */
   129	  caveats: string[];
   130	}
   131	
   132	const ARMS: ArmId[] = ["a", "b", "c", "cP", "cNP", "nb", "ab", "d0", "d1E", "d1B"];
   133	
   134	export function freshReport(dev: DevReport, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): FreshReport {
   135	  if (!dev.wall.positiveControlPassed) {
   136	    // G3: the assay is insensitive; stop with no fresh run and say so.
   137	    const zeros = Array(p.freshSeeds.length).fill(0);
   138	    const inputs: CandidateInput[] = p.panel.map((c) => ({ id: `cluster-${c}`, status: "ok", invalidReasons: [], contrasts: { builder: zeros, poly: zeros, space: zeros, inoc: zeros, spaceP: zeros, spaceNP: zeros }, delta: p.delta[String(c)], survivedBlocks: 0, blocks: p.freshSeeds.length, polymerAdequate: true, expectedBlocks: p.freshSeeds.length }));
   139	    const results = decideCandidates(inputs, false);
   140	    return { purpose: "confirmation", seeds: p.freshSeeds, candidates: [], results, panel: panelVerdict(results), ran: false, caveats: ["The hand-built positive control showed no spatial dependence on dev, so no confirmation seed was run."] };
   141	  }
   142	  const candidates: FreshCandidate[] = p.panel.map((cluster) => {
   143	    const raw = p.freshSeeds.map((s) => compactBlock(runBlock(cluster, s, p)));
   144	    const devC = dev.candidates.find((c) => c.cluster === cluster)!;
   145	    const col = (k: keyof Contrasts) => raw.map((b) => (b.contrasts ? (b.contrasts[k] as number) : 0));
   146	    const validBlocks = raw.filter((b) => b.valid).length;
   147	    const reasons = raw.filter((b) => !b.valid).map((b) => `seed ${b.seed}: ${b.invalidReason}`);
   148	    const sat = raw.filter((b) => Object.values(b.outcomes).some((o) => o.saturated)).length;
   149	    const input: CandidateInput = {
   150	      id: `cluster-${cluster}`,
   151	      status: validBlocks === raw.length ? "ok" : "invalid",
   152	      invalidReasons: reasons,
   153	      contrasts: { builder: col("builder"), poly: col("poly"), space: col("space"), inoc: col("inoc"), spaceP: col("spaceP"), spaceNP: col("spaceNP") },
   154	      delta: p.delta[String(cluster)],
   155	      survivedBlocks: raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes.b?.survived).length,
   156	      blocks: validBlocks,
   157	      polymerAdequate: devC.polymerAdequate,
   158	      expectedBlocks: p.freshSeeds.length,
   159	    };
   160	    const medianY = Object.fromEntries(ARMS.map((a) => [a, medianOrNull(raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes[a]).map((b) => b.outcomes[a].y))]));
   161	    return {
   162	      cluster,
   163	      blocks: raw,
   164	      input,
   165	      medianY,
   166	      fluxAttribution: validBlocks === 0 ? { available: false, reason: "no valid block produced a measurement", growthAttributedBlocks: 0 } : sat === 0 ? { available: true, reason: "no role counter reached saturation in any block", growthAttributedBlocks: raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes.b?.growthAttributed).length } : { available: false, reason: `a role counter reached ${p.roleSaturation} in ${sat} block(s)`, growthAttributedBlocks: 0 },
   167	    };
   168	  });
   169	  const results = decideCandidates(candidates.map((c) => c.input), dev.wall.positiveControlPassed);
   170	  const bs = dev.wall.builderScale;
   171	  const caveats: string[] = [];
   172	  if (!(bs.W1.passes || bs.W2.passes)) caveats.push(`The hand-built wall at builder-scale polymer (${p.wall.builderScaleP}) was not detected on dev (W1 ${bs.W1.k}/${bs.W1.d.length}, W2 ${bs.W2.k}/${bs.W2.d.length} positive), so the assay is shown to see a structure ${Math.round(p.wall.totalP / p.wall.builderScaleP)} times builder scale and a null for builder-scale polymer is weak evidence of absence.`);
   173	  const noise = Math.max(...dev.candidates.map((c) => c.deltaProposed));
   174	  caveats.push(`Stage-2 replicate noise sets delta (largest ${noise}) against a receiver inoculum of ${p.reserve.B}: only a large effect could pass.`);
   175	  return { purpose: "confirmation", seeds: p.freshSeeds, candidates, results, panel: panelVerdict(results), ran: true, caveats };
   176	}
   177	
   178	export interface FixturesReport {
   179	  protocol: { grid: number; kernelRadius: number; T2: number; reserve: { B: number; E: number }; delta: number };
   180	  reports: FixtureReport[];
   181	  gift: Record<GiftVariant, number>;
   182	  guards: GuardResult[];
   183	}
   184	
   185	export function fixturesReport(): FixturesReport {
   186	  const fp = fixtureProtocol();
   187	  const delta = Math.ceil(fp.reserve.B / 50);
   188	  const reports: FixtureReport[] = [];
   189	  for (const v of ["uniform", "footprint", "annulus"] as const) reports.push(decideFixture(`energy-gift-${v}`, FIXTURE_SEEDS, FIXTURE_SEEDS.map((s) => giftBlock(v, s, fp)), delta));
   190	  reports.push(decideFixture("takeover-only", FIXTURE_SEEDS, FIXTURE_SEEDS.map((s) => takeoverBlock(s, fp)), delta));
   191	  reports.push(decideFixture("null-field", FIXTURE_SEEDS, FIXTURE_SEEDS.map((s) => nullBlock(s, fp)), delta));
   192	  return { protocol: { grid: fp.grid, kernelRadius: fp.kernelRadius, T2: fp.T2, reserve: fp.reserve, delta }, reports, gift: GIFT, guards: runGuards() };
   193	}
   194	
   195	/** Block, step and cost accounting of one regeneration (informational; timings are measured by the tool, never sealed). */
   196	export function stepProjection(p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): { formula: string; blocks: number; steps: number } {
   197	  const blocks = p.panel.length * (p.devSeeds.length + p.freshSeeds.length);
   198	  const devExtra = p.panel.length * p.devSeeds.length * 2 * p.T2;
   199	  const perBlock = 2 * p.T1 + 10 * p.T2;
   200	  return { formula: "blocks * (2*T1 + 10*T2) + devBlocks * 2*T2 + wall runs", blocks, steps: blocks * perBlock + devExtra + 4 * 2 * p.wallSeeds.length * p.T2 };
   201	}
   202	
   203	export interface Payload {
   204	  kind: "niche-evidence";
   205	  version: string;
   206	  protocol: Protocol;
   207	  protocolHash: string;
   208	  registryDigest: string;
   209	  stepProjection: ReturnType<typeof stepProjection>;
   210	  dev: DevReport;
   211	  fresh: FreshReport;
   212	  fixtures: FixturesReport;
   213	  limits: string[];
   214	  integritySha256: string;
   215	}
   216	
   217	export const LIMITS: readonly string[] = [
   218	  "Exploratory, CPU only (sim-ref), mutation off. No GPU evidence, no physics change, not part of the frozen preregistration.",
   219	  "A negative bounds this panel (3 builders, 1 receiver), these horizons, this field and this injury regime only; it does not say a second link is impossible.",
   220	  "Eight fresh blocks with exact sign tests and Holm across candidates can only certify an effect that is positive in all or nearly all blocks; a modest real effect reads as unsupported.",
   221	  "The inoculum-matched controls screen resource totals delivered as inoculum; they do not prove that no spatial resource effect exists.",
   222	  "The ablation arm is analytical (polymer converted to waste at transfer, released energy booked as named heat): it tests whether the polymer itself carries the benefit, not the pinned in-sim polymerTransport ablation.",
   223	  "kGene maintenance is not separable from catalytic and ordinary maintenance in the reference step and is reported unavailable.",
   224	  "Receiver-labelled biomass inventory can rise by relabelling existing detritus; growth is claimed only where flux attribution is available and synthesis is at least half the gain.",
   225	  "Builder polymer at this scale is far below the hand-wall control's; the builder-scale wall result is reported, not gating.",
   226	];
   227	
   228	export function sealPayload(body: Omit<Payload, "integritySha256">): Payload {
   229	  const integritySha256 = sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON(body)));
   230	  return { ...body, integritySha256 };
   231	}
   232	export function verifySeal(p: Payload): boolean {
   233	  const { integritySha256, ...body } = p;
   234	  return integritySha256 === sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON(body)));
   235	}
   236	
   237	export function buildPayload(registryDigest: string, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Payload {
   238	  const dev = devReport(p);
   239	  const fresh = freshReport(dev, p);
   240	  return sealPayload({ kind: "niche-evidence", version: EVIDENCE_VERSION, protocol: p, protocolHash: protocolHash(registryDigest, p), registryDigest, stepProjection: stepProjection(p), dev, fresh, fixtures: fixturesReport(), limits: [...LIMITS] });
   241	}

     4	 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --bench          step rate on this machine and the projected regeneration time (informational)
     5	 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --calibrate      dev seeds only: writes calibration.json (delta floors, polymer adequacy, hand-wall G3)
     6	 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --freeze         write protocol.lock.json (protocol hash, seed lists, registry digest) BEFORE any fresh run
     7	 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --write          write evidence.json and manifest.json (once, deliberately; needs a matching lock)
     8	 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --check          validate the saved artifacts without simulating
     9	 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --replay-check   regenerate the payload and require byte equality

    26	let hooked = false;
    27	function registerAliases(): void {
    28	  if (hooked || process.env.VITEST) return;
    29	  hooked = true;
    30	  try {
    31	    registerHooks({
    32	      resolve(specifier, context, next) {
    33	        const m = /^@bl\/(.+)$/.exec(specifier);
    34	        return m ? next(pathToFileURL(`${REPO}packages/${m[1]}/src/index.ts`).href, context) : next(specifier, context);
    35	      },
    36	    });
    37	  } catch { /* already running under a loader that resolves @bl/* (vitest) */ }
    38	}

    40	const lib = async () => {
    41	  registerAliases();
    42	  const [evidence, protocol, schema, decision, assay] = await Promise.all([
    43	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/evidence.ts"),
    44	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/protocol.ts"),
    45	    import("../../packages/schema/src/index.ts"),
    46	    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/decision.ts"),
