    51	
    52	export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
    53	export const sha256 = async (text: string | Uint8Array): Promise<string> => {
    54	  const { schema } = await lib();
    55	  return schema.sha256HexJS(typeof text === "string" ? new TextEncoder().encode(text) : text);
    56	};
    57	export const registryDigest = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => sha256(readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`));
    58	
    59	/** Files whose bytes feed the evidence: the library, the simulator and schema it reads, and this tool. */
    60	const SOURCE_DIRS = [SRC_DIR, "packages/schema/src/", "packages/sim-ref/src/"];
    61	export function sourceFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
    62	  const dirs = SOURCE_DIRS.flatMap((d) => readdirSync(`${root}${d}`).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts")).sort().map((f) => d + f));
    63	  return [...dirs, "tools/niche/niche.ts"];
    64	}
    65	
    66	export async function generate(): Promise<unknown> {
    67	  const L = await lib();
    68	  return L.buildPayload(await registryDigest());
    69	}
    70	
    71	export async function makeManifest(payload: any, evidenceText: string, seconds: number): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    72	  const sources: Record<string, string> = {};
    73	  for (const f of sourceFiles()) sources[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(REPO + f));
    74	  return {
    75	    kind: "niche-evidence-manifest",
    76	    payloadFileSha256: await sha256(evidenceText),
    77	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
    78	    protocolHash: payload.protocolHash,
    79	    registryDigest: payload.registryDigest,
    80	    stepProjection: payload.stepProjection,
    81	    sources,
    82	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", baseCommit: BASE_COMMIT, node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, regenerationSeconds: Math.round(seconds), generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    83	  };
    84	}
    85	
    86	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
    87	export async function checkSaved(paths = { evidence: EVIDENCE, manifest: MANIFEST, calibration: CALIBRATION, lock: LOCK }, root = REPO): Promise<string[]> {
    88	  const L = await lib();
    89	  const problems: string[] = [];
    90	  for (const f of Object.values(paths)) if (!existsSync(f)) problems.push(`${f} is missing`);
    91	  if (problems.length) return problems;
    92	  const text = readFileSync(paths.evidence, "utf8");
    93	  const payload = JSON.parse(text);
    94	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.manifest, "utf8"));
    95	  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.lock, "utf8"));
    96	  const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.calibration, "utf8"));
    97	  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
    98	  if (!L.verifySeal(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
    99	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
   100	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
   101	  if (manifest.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("manifest protocolHash differs from the payload");
   102	  // The protocol was frozen (lock written) before the fresh run, and nothing changed since.
   103	  const digest = await registryDigest(root);
   104	  if (payload.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("seed-registry.json changed since the evidence was written");
   105	  if (lock.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("protocol.lock.json does not match the payload protocol hash");
   106	  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHash(digest, L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from the frozen lock");
   107	  if (canon(payload.protocol) !== canon(L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("payload protocol differs from the protocol in code");
   108	  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon({ dev: L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, replicates: L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds, wall: L.PROTOCOL.wallSeeds, fresh: L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds })) problems.push("lock seed lists differ from the protocol");
   109	  // Dev results are reported separately and match the calibration file the protocol was frozen on.
   110	  if (canon(calibration) !== canon(payload.dev)) problems.push("calibration.json differs from the dev section of evidence.json");
   111	  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(paths.calibration)))) problems.push("lock calibrationSha256 does not match calibration.json");
   112	  for (const c of payload.dev.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) problems.push(`delta for cluster ${c.cluster} (${L.PROTOCOL.delta[String(c.cluster)]}) is not the dev replicate floor (${c.deltaProposed})`);
   113	  // Seed hygiene.
   114	  const registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`, "utf8")).reservations as { first: number; last: number; owner: string }[];
   115	  const all = [...L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.wallSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds].map((seed, i) => ({ id: `s${i}`, seed }));
   116	  const own = "i06 niche construction";
   117	  const foreign = L.schema.seedCollisions(all, registry.filter((r) => !r.owner.startsWith(own)), own);
   118	  for (const f of foreign) problems.push(`seed collision: ${f}`);
   119	  // Decision recomputation from the stored inputs.
   120	  const recomputed = payload.fresh.ran === false ? payload.fresh.results : L.decideCandidates(payload.fresh.candidates.map((c: any) => c.input), payload.dev.wall.positiveControlPassed);
   121	  if (canon(recomputed) !== canon(payload.fresh.results)) problems.push("fresh results do not follow the stated decision rules");
   122	  if (canon(L.panelVerdict(recomputed)) !== canon(payload.fresh.panel)) problems.push("panel verdict does not follow the stated rules");
   123	  // Every ledger exactly zero.
   124	  const blocks = [...payload.dev.candidates, ...payload.fresh.candidates].flatMap((c: any) => c.blocks);
   125	  for (const b of blocks) for (const [arm, recs] of Object.entries<any[]>(b.transfers)) for (const r of recs) if (r.residual !== "0" || r.matterResidual !== "0") problems.push(`block ${b.seed} arm ${arm} op ${r.op}: nonzero residual`);
   126	  // Manifest sources.
   127	  const listed = Object.keys(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>);
   128	  if (existsSync(root + SRC_DIR)) for (const f of sourceFiles(root)) if (!listed.includes(f)) problems.push(`source ${f} is not in the manifest (added since the evidence was written)`);
   129	  for (const [file, hash] of Object.entries<string>(manifest.sources)) {
   130	    if (!existsSync(root + file)) problems.push(`source ${file} is missing`);
   131	    else if ((await sha256(readFileSync(root + file))) !== hash) problems.push(`source ${file} changed since the evidence was written`);
   132	  }
   133	  return problems;
   134	}
   135	
   136	export async function replayCheck(evidencePath = EVIDENCE): Promise<string[]> {
   137	  const fresh = serialize(await generate());
   138	  return fresh === readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
   139	}
   140	
   141	async function bench(): Promise<void> {
   142	  const L = await lib();
   143	  const t0 = Date.now();
   144	  const r = L.runBlock(L.PROTOCOL.panel[0], L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds[0], L.PROTOCOL, L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds[0]);
   145	  const ms = Date.now() - t0;
   146	  const steps = 2 * L.PROTOCOL.T1 + 12 * L.PROTOCOL.T2;
   147	  const proj = L.stepProjection();
   148	  console.log(`block valid=${r.valid}: ${steps} steps in ${ms} ms = ${(ms / steps).toFixed(2)} ms/step`);
   149	  console.log(`projected regeneration: ${proj.steps} steps (${proj.formula}) ~ ${Math.round((proj.steps * ms) / steps / 1000)} s`);
   150	}
   151	
   152	async function main(): Promise<void> {
   153	  const mode = process.argv[2];
   154	  if (mode === "--bench") return bench();
   155	  if (mode === "--calibrate") {
   156	    const L = await lib();
   157	    const dev = L.devReport();
   158	    writeFileSync(CALIBRATION, serialize(dev));
   159	    for (const c of dev.candidates) console.log(`cluster ${c.cluster}: delta ${c.deltaProposed} builder P median ${c.builderPMedian} adequate ${c.polymerAdequate}`);
   160	    console.log(`hand-wall: W1 k=${dev.wall.W1.k} passes=${dev.wall.W1.passes}; W2 k=${dev.wall.W2.k} passes=${dev.wall.W2.passes}; positive control ${dev.wall.positiveControlPassed ? "passed" : "FAILED"} (${dev.wall.used})`);
   161	    console.log("wrote calibration.json (dev only). Copy the deltas into PROTOCOL.delta, then --freeze.");
   162	    return;
   163	  }
   164	  if (mode === "--freeze") {
   165	    const L = await lib();
   166	    if (!existsSync(CALIBRATION)) throw new Error("run --calibrate first");
   167	    if (existsSync(LOCK) && !process.argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("protocol.lock.json exists; pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   168	    const digest = await registryDigest();
   169	    const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CALIBRATION, "utf8"));
   170	    for (const c of calibration.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) throw new Error(`PROTOCOL.delta[${c.cluster}] must be ${c.deltaProposed} (the dev replicate floor)`);
   171	    const lock = { kind: "niche-protocol-lock", protocolHash: L.protocolHash(digest, L.PROTOCOL), registryDigest: digest, seeds: { dev: L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, replicates: L.PROTOCOL.devReplicateSeeds, wall: L.PROTOCOL.wallSeeds, fresh: L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds }, calibrationSha256: await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION)), note: "written before the fresh-seed run; fresh results never tune the protocol", frozenAt: new Date().toISOString() };
   172	    writeFileSync(LOCK, serialize(lock));
   173	    console.log(`wrote protocol.lock.json ${lock.protocolHash}`);
   174	    return;
   175	  }
   176	  if (mode === "--write") {
   177	    if (existsSync(EVIDENCE) && !process.argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("evidence.json exists; files are written once. Pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   178	    if (!existsSync(LOCK)) throw new Error("no protocol.lock.json: run --calibrate and --freeze before the fresh run");
   179	    const L = await lib();
   180	    const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(LOCK, "utf8"));
   181	    if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHash(await registryDigest(), L.PROTOCOL)) throw new Error("the protocol in code differs from protocol.lock.json");
   182	    const t0 = Date.now();
   183	    const payload = (await generate()) as any;
   184	    const seconds = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
   185	    const text = serialize(payload);
   186	    const manifestText = serialize(await makeManifest(payload, text, seconds));
   187	    writeFileSync(`${EVIDENCE}.tmp`, text);
   188	    writeFileSync(`${MANIFEST}.tmp`, manifestText);
   189	    renameSync(`${EVIDENCE}.tmp`, EVIDENCE);
   190	    renameSync(`${MANIFEST}.tmp`, MANIFEST);
   191	    console.log(`wrote evidence.json (${text.length} bytes) seal ${payload.integritySha256} in ${seconds.toFixed(0)} s; verdict: ${payload.fresh.panel.verdict}`);
   192	    return;
   193	  }
   194	  if (mode === "--check" || mode === "--replay-check") {
   195	    const t0 = Date.now();
   196	    const problems = mode === "--check" ? await checkSaved() : await replayCheck();
   197	    if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   198	    console.log(`${mode} ok${mode === "--replay-check" ? ` (${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)} s)` : ""}`);
   199	    return;
   200	  }
   201	  console.error("usage: node tools/niche/niche.ts --bench | --calibrate | --freeze | --write | --check | --replay-check");
   202	  process.exit(2);
   203	}
   204	if (process.argv[1] && pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href === import.meta.url) await main();

     1	// Pure helpers for the niche-construction page: formatting, labels, table rows. No DOM lookups; the page reads evidence.json only.
     2	export interface Rational { num: number; den: number }
     3	export const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c]!);
     4	export const fmt = (n: number) => n.toLocaleString('en-US');
     5	export const ratText = (r: Rational): string => (r.num === r.den ? '1' : `${r.num}/${r.den}`);
     6	
     7	export type EdgeState = 'supported' | 'unsupported' | 'unavailable';
     8	export interface EdgeView { state: EdgeState; word: string; text: string }
     9	
    10	/** Evidence label -> the edge drawn between builder and receiver. A technical failure or a limited measurement is "unavailable", never "unsupported". */
    11	export function edgeOf(label: string, mechanism: string): EdgeView {
    12	  if (label === 'supported') return { state: 'supported', word: 'supported', text: `Dependency edge supported for this pair, horizon and field (${mechanism}).` };
    13	  if (label === 'unsupported-within-tested-domain') return { state: 'unsupported', word: 'unsupported', text: 'No causal benefit detected within the tested domain. This does not say a second link is impossible.' };
    14	  if (label === 'not-run') return { state: 'unavailable', word: 'unavailable', text: 'Not run (runtime rule). Not a negative result.' };
    15	  return { state: 'unavailable', word: 'unavailable', text: 'The assay could not decide (invalid blocks or an insensitive measurement). Not a negative result.' };
    16	}

    18	export const ARM_TEXT: Record<string, { name: string; what: string }> = {
    19	  a: { name: 'Co-culture', what: 'The builder is still alive when the receiver arrives.' },
    20	  b: { name: 'Builder removed', what: 'Living cells are relabelled non-living; the whole conditioned field stays in place.' },
    21	  c: { name: 'Matched inventory, scrambled', what: 'Same totals of every channel as the builder-removed field, structure shuffled.' },
    22	  cP: { name: 'Polymer only scrambled', what: 'Only the polymer is shuffled; everything else stays where it was.' },
    23	  cNP: { name: 'Everything but polymer scrambled', what: 'All other channels are shuffled; the polymer stays where it was.' },
    24	  nb: { name: 'Nonbuilder history', what: 'Same founders and budget, without the building gene, then removed.' },
    25	  ab: { name: 'Ablation', what: 'Builder cost kept; its polymer is turned into waste at the transfer, the released energy booked as named heat.' },
    26	  d0: { name: 'Receiver alone', what: 'The same total budget as an unconditioned lattice, same inoculum.' },
    27	  d1E: { name: 'Receiver alone + field energy', what: 'Receiver alone with the free energy the field held near the inoculum handed over as inoculum.' },

    39	export interface ArmRow { id: string; name: string; what: string; median: number; perBlock: number[] }
    40	export function armRows(blocks: { outcomes: Record<string, { y: number }> }[], medianY: Record<string, number>): ArmRow[] {
    41	  return ARM_ORDER.filter((id) => id in medianY).map((id) => ({ id, ...ARM_TEXT[id], median: medianY[id], perBlock: blocks.map((b) => b.outcomes[id]?.y ?? 0) }));

    45	export function ledgerRows(startTotals: Record<string, { matter: string; energy: string; reserveB: number; reserveE: number }>, transfers: Record<string, { residual: string; matterResidual: string }[]>): LedgerRow[] {
    46	  return ARM_ORDER.filter((id) => id in startTotals).map((arm) => {
    47	    const t = startTotals[arm];
    48	    const bad = (transfers[arm] ?? []).some((r) => r.residual !== '0' || r.matterResidual !== '0');
    49	    return { arm, matter: t.matter, energy: t.energy, reserve: `B ${t.reserveB}, E ${t.reserveE}`, residual: bad ? 'NONZERO' : '0' };
    50	  });
    51	}

# I06 niche construction, first causal rung: result

Exploratory, CPU only (sim-ref), mutation off, no physics change. Evidence: `evidence.json` (sealed), `manifest.json`, `calibration.json` (dev only), `protocol.lock.json` (written before the fresh run). Page: `apps/lab/niche/` (local only; 404 on the deployed site).

## Result

**No causal benefit detected** for any of the three builder candidates (M3 founder clusters 0, 4, 9 with the BUILD gene, receiver cluster 12), 8 fresh blocks each, T1 = 60 conditioning steps, T2 = 80 assay steps, 24x24 lattice with recurring injury and polymer shell. Candidate p = 255/256, 255/256, 1 (needed at most 1/60 after Holm); in every candidate the "inoculum matched" contrast is negative (the receiver does better when handed the matter or energy the field held than in the conditioned field), the polymer-ablation and spatial contrasts are not consistently positive, and the receiver survived in 8/8 blocks in every arm family.

## What this does and does not say

- The assay is not blind: the hand-built wall at 1200 polymer (about 10x what builders leave) was seen on dev (W2 floor under the receiver, 7/8 positive, p 9/256), so spatial structure can be detected at all. At builder scale (120) the same wall was not detected (W2 3/8, W1 4/8), and Stage-2 replicate noise sets delta at 291-404 against an inoculum of 480. A null for builder-scale polymer is therefore weak evidence of absence. Stated in the page and in `fresh.caveats`.
- Builder polymer at the end of conditioning is only about 100-120 (adequate by the pre-stated floor of 10).
- Flux attribution was available in every block (no role counter saturated); growth (synthesis at least half the gain) was attributed in 1, 1 and 2 of 8 builder-removed blocks, so the receiver-biomass differences are mostly inventory, not growth.
- Builder cost is reported from census totals; `kGene` maintenance is `unavailable` (not separable in the reference step).
- Negative fixture: energy-gift worlds (uniform, inside the footprint, in the halo annulus) are fooled by the naive metric (8/8 blocks positive) and caught by the scramble (uniform) or the energy-matched inoculum (footprint, annulus); takeover-only raises receiver-labelled inventory with zero synthesis; the null field gives exact zeros; the four placement/pool/underfunding guards refuse as designed.
- Exploratory, one receiver genome, one field per candidate; not part of the frozen preregistration; not a statement that a second link is impossible.

## Pre-stated rules and implementation amendments

Decision rules are PLAN 5; they were not changed. Amendments made before any fresh run: d1B bound matter is B+P (see REVIEW finding 1); the positive control uses 8 dev wall seeds (an exact sign test over 4 blocks cannot reach 1/20); the hand-wall is qualified with injury on (the floor protects only under `injuryShell`) and at 10x builder polymer; the bound `PROTOCOL.delta` values are the dev replicate floors, checked by `--check`. After the Sol source review the evidence was regenerated (REVIEW.md); the verdict did not change.

## Commands and results (this worktree, Node 24.2, machine at load average about 60)

    const F = new Array<number>(FLUX_COUNT).fill(0);
    const x = new Int32Array(NN_I);
    const h = new Int32Array(NN_H);
    const o = new Int32Array(NN_O);
    const wb = new Int8Array(NN_BYTES);
    const mutCap = c.mutRate === 0 ? 0 : divu(U32_MAX, c.mutRate);
    // Recurring injury (WorldConfig.injuryPeriod; absent = RULE_VERSION 1).
    const injuryStep = c.injuryPeriod !== undefined && (step + 1) % c.injuryPeriod === 0;
    // Capability genes (WorldConfig.genes; absent = RULE_VERSION 1): each gene in the set gates one
    // reaction (DECOMP decomposition, BUILD polymer building), every carried gene costs kGene, and a
    // share of mutation events flips a gene bit. Without genes every mask is 0 and every gate open.
    const genes = c.genes !== undefined;
    const kGene = c.kGene ?? 0;
    const geneFlip = c.geneFlip ?? 0;
    const geneDrive = c.geneDrive ?? 0;
    const masks = geneMasks(c);

    for (let yy = 0; yy < this.H; yy++) {
      for (let xx = 0; xx < this.W; xx++) {
        const i = yy * this.W + xx;
        const base = cellBase(c.seed, step, i);
        let A = cells[CH.A * n + i];
        let B = cells[CH.B * n + i];
        let C = cells[CH.C * n + i];
        let P = cells[CH.P * n + i];
        let E = cells[CH.E * n + i];
        let S = cells[CH.S * n + i];
        let mot = MOT_ZERO;
        const L = this.light(xx, yy, step);
        let heat = 0;
        let lightIn = 0;
        let newB = 0;
        F.fill(0);
        const living = this.living(genome, i);
        // Pool caps (see POOL_MAX): excess free energy or signal leaves as heat.
        if (E > POOL_MAX) {
          heat += E - POOL_MAX;
          E = POOL_MAX;
        }
        if (S > POOL_MAX) {
          heat += S - POOL_MAX;
          S = POOL_MAX;
        }

        if (living && B > 0) {
          // Unpack weights.
          const ref = this.refWords;
          for (let b = 0; b < NN_BYTES; b++) {
            const word = ref ? ref[G.W0 + (b >> 2)] : genome[(G.W0 + (b >> 2)) * n + i];
            const byte = (word >>> ((b & 3) * 8)) & 0xff;
            wb[b] = byte > 127 ? byte - 256 : byte;
          }
          const sat = (v: number) => (v > 127 ? 127 : v);
          const cap24 = (v: number) => (v > 0xffffff ? 0xffffff : v);
          const Se = cap24(cells[CH.S * n + this.nb(xx, yy, 1, 0)]);
          const Sw = cap24(cells[CH.S * n + this.nb(xx, yy, -1, 0)]);
          const Ss = cap24(cells[CH.S * n + this.nb(xx, yy, 0, 1)]);
          const Sn = cap24(cells[CH.S * n + this.nb(xx, yy, 0, -1)]);
          x[0] = sat(A >>> 2);
          x[1] = sat(B >>> 2);
          x[2] = sat(C >>> 2);
          x[3] = sat(P >>> 2);
          x[4] = sat(divu(mulu(cap24(E), 16), addu(B, 1)));
          x[5] = L >>> 1;
          x[6] = sat(S >>> 2);
          const sg = c.signalGain ?? 1;
          x[7] = clampi(divi(muli(Se - Sw, sg), 4), -127, 127);
          x[8] = clampi(divi(muli(Ss - Sn, sg), 4), -127, 127);
          x[9] = clampi(divi(this.U[i], 2), -127, 127);
          controllerForward(wb, x, h, o);
          const r =(k: number) => (o[k] > 0 ? o[k] : 0);
          mot = ((o[OUT.MX] + 128) | ((o[OUT.MY] + 128) << 8)) >>> 0;

          // Photosynthesis A + light -> B.
          const K = c.kCatHalf;
          let t = mulShr(cat(B, K), r(OUT.PHOTO), 7);
          t = mulShr(t, L, 8);
          let q = Math.min(A, mulFrac(t, c.kPhoto, 12, draw(base, RND.PHOTO)));
          A -= q; B += q; newB += q; F[FX.photo] = q;
          lightIn = q * (c.eB - c.eA);
          // Respiration B -> C + energy.
          t = mulShr(cat(B, K), r(OUT.RESP), 7);
          q = Math.min(B, mulFrac(t, c.kResp, 12, draw(base, RND.RESP)));
          B -= q; C += q; E += q * (c.eB - c.eC); F[FX.resp] = q;
          if (E > POOL_MAX) {
            heat += E - POOL_MAX;
            E = POOL_MAX;
          }
          // Under WorldConfig.genes only a cell carrying a reaction's gene has the reaction (and its
          // output in the work sum below); neutral is excluded with genes, so the cell's own genome
          // word is the one to read.
          const word = genome[G.PARAM1 * n + i];
          const decomp = masks.decomp === 0 || (word & masks.decomp) !== 0;
          const build = masks.build === 0 || (word & masks.build) !== 0;
          // Gene v2 (WorldConfig.geneDrive): a carrier of the build gene builds at least at the
          // floor whatever its weights say; max with 0 is the output itself, so absent is Gene v1.
          const rBuild = (word & masks.build) !== 0 ? Math.max(r(OUT.BUILD), geneDrive) : r(OUT.BUILD);
          // Decomposition C -> A + energy (gated by the DECOMP gene).
          if (decomp) {
            t = mulShr(cat(B, K), r(OUT.DECOMP), 7);
            q = Math.min(C, mulFrac(t, c.kDecomp, 12, draw(base, RND.DECOMP)));
            C -= q; A += q; E += q * (c.eC - c.eA); F[FX.decomp] = q;
            if (E > POOL_MAX) {
              heat += E - POOL_MAX;
              E = POOL_MAX;
            }
          }
          // Growth A + energy -> B.
          let g = c.eB - c.eA;
          t = mulShr(cat(B, K), r(OUT.GROW), 7);
          q = Math.min(A, divu(E, g), mulFrac(t, c.kGrow, 12, draw(base, RND.GROW)));
          A -= q; B += q; E -= q * g; newB += q; F[FX.grow] = q;
          // Build polymer B + energy -> P (gated by the BUILD gene).
          if (build) {
            g = c.eP - c.eB;
            t = mulShr(cat(B, K), rBuild, 7);
            q = Math.min(B, divu(E, g), mulFrac(t, c.kBuild, 12, draw(base, RND.BUILD)));
            B -= q; P += q; E -= q * g; F[FX.build] = q;
          }
          // Signal emission E -> S.
          t = mulShr(cat(B, K), r(OUT.EMIT), 7);
          q = Math.min(E, mulFrac(t, c.kEmit, 12, draw(base, RND.EMIT)));
          E -= q; S += q; F[FX.emit] = q;
          if (S > POOL_MAX) {
            heat += S - POOL_MAX;
            S = POOL_MAX;
          }
          // Work of catalysis + maintenance (+ each carried gene's maintenance under WorldConfig.genes:
          // one draw, the term times the count, so kGene stays below mulFrac's 2^16; bound: the work
          // term is < 2^29, kMaint's <= B <= 2^26 and the gene term <= 8 * 2^26 = 2^29, so the sum < 2^31).
          const sumr = r(0) + r(1) + (decomp ? r(2) : 0) + r(3) + (build ? rBuild : 0) + r(5);
          t = mulShr(cat(B, K), sumr, 7);
          let cost = mulFrac(t, c.kCost, 16, draw(base, RND.COST)) + mulFrac(B, c.kMaint, 16, draw(base, RND.MAINT));
          if (genes) {
            const carried = popcount(word & masks.set);
            if (carried > 0) cost += carried * mulFrac(B, kGene, 16, draw(base, RND.GENE));
          }
          if (E >= cost) {
            E -= cost;
            heat += cost;
          } else {
            const deficit = cost - E;
            heat += E;
            E = 0;
            q = Math.min(B, deficit);
            B -= q; C += q; F[FX.starve] = q;
            heat += q * (c.eB - c.eC);
          }

          // Mutation, at a rate per newly synthesised quantum.
          if (newB > 0 && mutCap > 0) {
            const p = mutationThreshold(newB, mutCap, c.mutRate);
            if (draw(base, RND.MUT) < p) {
              const parentHi = genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i];
              const parentLo = genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i];
              // Under WorldConfig.genes a share of events flips a gene bit instead of nudging a weight.
              if (genes && draw(base, RND.GENE_FLIP) < geneFlip) flipGene(genome, n, i, c, draw(base, RND.MUT_WHICH));
              else mutateInPlace(genome, n, i, c, draw(base, RND.MUT_WHICH), draw(base, RND.MUT_DELTA));
              const childLo = packLineageLo(c, i);
              genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] = (step + 1) >>> 0;
              genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] = childLo;
              events.push({ childHi: step + 1, childLo, parentHi, parentLo });
            }
          }
        }

        // Passive processes (all cells).
        let q = mulFrac(P, c.kPDecay, 16, draw(base, RND.PDECAY));
        P -= q; C += q; heat += q * (c.eP - c.eC); F[FX.pdecay] = q;
        q = mulFrac(B, c.kBDecay, 16, draw(base, RND.BDECAY));
        B -= q; C += q; heat += q * (c.eB - c.eC); F[FX.bdecay] = q;
        q = mulFrac(E, c.kELeak, 16, draw(base, RND.ELEAK));
        E -= q; heat += q;
        q = mulFrac(S, c.kSDecay, 16, draw(base, RND.SDECAY));
        S -= q; heat += q;
        const t = mulFrac(C, L, 8, draw(base, RND.ABIO1));
        q = Math.min(C, mulFrac(t, c.kAbio, 16, draw(base, RND.ABIO2)));
        C -= q; A += q; heat += q * (c.eC - c.eA); F[FX.abio] = q;

        // Injury: the cell's bound structure is destroyed as by applyLesion
        // (the lineage is cleared just below, since B and P are now 0).
        let shelled = false;
        if (injuryStep && this.injured(xx, yy, step)) {
          const shell = c.injuryShell;
          if (shell !== undefined && P >= shell) {
            // Polymer shell: the wound takes exactly `shell` polymer; B, E, motility and lineage survive
            // (even when the shell was the last polymer: the empty-cell cleanup below skips it).
            P -= shell; C += shell; heat += shell * (c.eP - c.eC);
            shelled = true;
          } else {
            heat += B * (c.eB - c.eC) + P * (c.eP - c.eC) + E;
            C += B + P;
            B = 0;
            P = 0;
            E = 0;
            mot = MOT_ZERO;
          }
        }

        if (living && B === 0 && P === 0 && !shelled) {
          genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] = 0;
          genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] = 0;
        }
        out[CH.A * n + i] = A;
        out[CH.B * n + i] = B;
        out[CH.C * n + i] = C;
        out[CH.P * n + i] = P;
        out[CH.E * n + i] = E;
        out[CH.S * n + i] = S;
        out[CH.MOT * n + i] = mot;
        lightTotal += BigInt(lightIn);
        heatAcc += heat;
        if (heatAcc > 2 ** 50) {
          heatTotal += BigInt(heatAcc);
          heatAcc = 0;
        }
        for (let k = 0; k < FLUX_COUNT; k++) fluxTotal[k] += F[k];
        const sat16 = (v: number) => (v > 0xffff ? 0xffff : v);
        this.roles[i * ROLE_WORDS] = (sat16(F[FX.photo]) | (sat16(F[FX.grow]) << 16)) >>> 0;
        this.roles[i * ROLE_WORDS + 1] = (sat16(F[FX.decomp]) | (sat16(F[FX.resp]) << 16)) >>> 0;
      }
    }
    const oc = this.state.cells;
    this.state.cells = out;
    this.cellsB = oc;
    15	1. **Evidence fixture with a negative case that fools the naive metric** (section 4). Naive metric: "receiver biomass after conditioning exceeds receiver biomass without conditioning". Negative fixture `energy-gift`: a field that differs from the control only by extra stored free energy E (no polymer, no structure). Naive metric reports a large "benefit"; the matched-inventory and inoculum-matched controls reproduce it, so the edge is labelled unsupported. Positive fixture `hand-wall`: a hand-built polymer structure (known history, no builder involved) whose benefit disappears when the same polymer inventory is scrambled, to show the assay can see spatial dependence at all; it is qualified at the frozen scientific scale on dev (G3), not only at unit-test scale. Further fixtures: `null-field` (identical start in both arms, zero effect), `takeover-only` (no synthesis possible, yet receiver-labelled biomass rises by relabelling existing detritus: the naive inventory metric is fooled a second way).
    16	2. **Small CPU path.** An exact transfer module (ledger, relabel, inoculum placement, scramble), four-arm assay runner on `RefSim`, decision function, evidence sealing. Library in `packages/runner/src/niche/` (no new package, so no alias-map edits; the tool resolves `@bl/*` with the same `registerHooks` shim as `tools/predictability/atlas.ts`). Observers are read-only; a plain-vs-instrumented twin test shows the readout does not change physics.
    17	3. **Manifest and machine-readable evidence.** `docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/evidence.json` and `manifest.json`, `--write` / `--check` / `--replay-check`.
    18	4. **Working local page** `apps/lab/niche/index.html`, kept off the deployed site like `/predictability/`: the arms side by side per candidate, ledgers, effect sizes with intervals, dependency edge drawn supported / unsupported / unavailable, plus the negative fixture panel. Reads `evidence.json` only; runs no simulation.
    19	5. Docs: this PLAN, `REVIEW.md` (Sol dispositions), `EVIDENCE.md` (human result, written after the run).
    20	
    21	## 3. Reuse and the honest limits of reuse
    22	
    23	- **World, state and ledger:** `defaultConfig`, `allocState`/`buildWorld` helpers, `validateState`, `totalsOf`, `ledgerResidual`, `stateHash` from `@bl/schema`; `RefSim` from `@bl/sim-ref`. Existing sim-ref physics already makes P do something: polymer gates diffusion of A and C (`De = D*gateK/(gateK+P_t+P_n)`), is the substrate of adhesion, and shields from wounds under `injuryShell`. The builder is a carrier of the existing `genes: "ladder"` BUILD bit (`geneMasks`, optional `geneDrive` floor); the nonbuilder is the same genome with only the decomposition bit. Both keys stay out of `defaultConfig()`; no `WorldConfig` key is added at all.
    24	- **Discovery line:** reuse its vocabulary and gate shapes, not its runner. `EVIDENCE_STATUSES` ("supported", "unsupported-within-tested-domain", "invalid-or-incomplete", "unsupported-measurement") label every edge and fixture; `CampaignPurpose` names (`calibration`, `confirmation`) name the dev and fresh splits; `sha256HexJS` from `@bl/schema` hash evidence. `canonicalJSON` there accepts only safe integers (checked), so every sealed number is an integer or a rational `{num,den}` of integers; BigInt values are decimal strings; no floats appear in sealed payloads. Cases are not run through `runCase`/the coordinator: this is a transfer assay between two stages, which `FixtureSpec`/`InitialRecipe` cannot express without a runner change, and a runner change is out of scope. Stated as a limit, with the DESIGN section 2 control list as the checklist (3.3).
    25	- **Founders:** builder and receiver genomes are taken from `M3_FOUNDERS` (committed, `m3-50886563ec90fb39`), by the pre-stated panel rule in 5.1, never by outcome.
    26	- **Not touched:** `vendor/construction-renewal-v1` (D5 pin), `polymerTransport` (exists only in the pinned rule-2 revision, not in `main`), frozen preregistration, `RULE_VERSION`, golden pins, `docs/rules.md` bounds (the module adds no arithmetic to shared sim state; all of it is analytical, integer, outside the step function).
    27	- **Cost-retaining ablation without a rule change.** The pinned in-sim ablation (`polymerTransport: false`) is unavailable on `main`. The ablation arm is analytical: take the builder's field at the end of conditioning, so builder cost (consumed A, spent E, maintenance, genes) is retained, and convert its structural polymer to waste C inside the transfer, with the released energy `(eP-eC)*P` booked as a named analytical heat term `heatAnalytic`. Matter conserved, energy residual still exactly zero including `heatAnalytic`. It tests "does the polymer itself carry the benefit", not "the pinned ablation". Stated limit; the pinned ablation stays a future item.
    28	
    29	## 4. Design
    30	
    31	### 4.1 Worlds and stages
    32	
    33	Single tile, `tileW=tileH=24` (fixed; no 32x32, for the cost budget in G6), `mutRate=0`, `genes:"ladder"`, `kGene`, `geneFlip:0`, `geneDrive` and light/nutrient from dev calibration, `injury*` absent unless calibration shows polymer is otherwise inert (then `injuryPeriod/Radius/Prob/Shell` set identically in all arms; this is an explicit protocol choice, recorded). `T1` and `T2` are each at most 120. Two stages:
    34	
    35	- **Stage 1 (conditioning),** `T1` steps from a **declared total budget** `(matter0, energy0)`: nutrient A everywhere, builder (or nonbuilder) founder cells in a fixed source patch, and a **receiver reserve** `R = (B_r, E_r)` carried off-lattice from step 0 and inert during Stage 1. Accounting follows `packages/schema/src/accounting.ts`, where bound matter B is valued at `eB` per quantum and A at `eA`: funding the reserve removes `B_r` quanta of lattice A (footprint-free cells, raster order), and removes from lattice E exactly `(eB-eA)*B_r + E_r`, so that `reserveEnergy = eB*B_r + E_r` is paid for exactly. Matter: `lattice matter + B_r` is the same in every arm and in the receiver-alone controls. The audited invariant is **lattice plus reserve plus cumulative analytic heat**, cumulative across both stages: `ledgerResidual({energy: energy0}, state) + reserveEnergy + cumulativeHeatAnalytic === 0n` (the helper itself counts neither external term, so the module wraps it and tests the wrapper). Insufficient lattice A or E to fund the reserve makes the block `invalid-or-incomplete`.
    36	- **Stage 2 (assay),** `T2` steps after the transfer, receiver cells tagged by a reserved lineage-id set disjoint from builder ids (mutation off keeps tags stable). One seed per block gives the sim seed for both stages. **Absolute clock:** every Stage 2 arm and control (including the receiver-alone ones) starts at absolute step `T1` with the same sim seed, so the counter-based PRNG keys and the `(step+1) % injuryPeriod` schedule coincide across arms; receiver-alone worlds are constructed and their step counter set to `T1` rather than run from 0. A test checks that every arm in a block sees the same injury steps and RNG keys.
    37	
    38	### 4.2 Arms (per candidate pair and seed block)
    39	
    40	Conditioning histories (Stage 1): `builder` (BUILD+decomp), `nonbuilder` (decomp only, same genome weights, same founders, same budget), `ablation` (builder history, polymer converted to C at transfer per 3).
    41	
    42	Transfers (Stage 2), applied to each history:
    43	
    44	- **(a) co-culture:** receiver inoculum placed, living cells kept.
    45	- **(b) builder-removed whole-field transfer:** living cells relabelled non-living: lineage and genome cleared, B/P/E/A/C/S kept in place (matter and energy unchanged, relabel recorded with before/after totals); then the inoculum is placed.
    46	- **(c) matched-inventory scramble:** (b), then each channel (A,B,C,P,E,S) independently permuted over all lattice cells by a seeded integer permutation that does not depend on the history (same permutation for the same seed). Every channel total is preserved exactly; structure is not. Called "scrambled", never "unconditioned". The scramble also destroys E/B ratios, signal gradients and their alignment with receivers (controller inputs), so (c) alone cannot say that polymer arrangement is the cause. Two attribution reconstructions therefore run on the builder history: **(cP) P-only scramble** (permute only P; every other channel stays in place) and **(cNP) non-P scramble** (permute everything except P). They label the edge's mechanism (see 5.3); they do not gate supported/unsupported.
    47	
    48	Controls independent of history:
    49	
    50	- **(d0) receiver-alone:** empty lattice with the same total matter/energy budget in uniform A (nothing conditioned), same inoculum.
    51	- **(d1E) energy-matched receiver-alone** and **(d1B) biomass-matched receiver-alone:** (d0) with, separately, (d1E) the receiver reserve free energy raised by `dE = max(0, sum E over region in (b) - sum E over region in (d0))`, or (d1B) the reserve B raised by `dB = max(0, sum (A+B+C+P) bound-matter excess over region)` with its chemical-energy debit `(eB-eA)*dB`; the region is the inoculum footprint **plus a halo of radius `h`** (halo declared in the protocol, so an energy gift in an annulus just outside the footprint is matched). The raise is taken from the lattice (debits recorded, totals unchanged, `validateState` and pool caps checked before writing). Inoculum placement is on empty cells, so the footprint itself has no bound matter by construction and the halo is what makes bound-matter matching non-vacuous. These are the "more initial stored energy / matter explains it" controls (DESIGN section 2 requires biomass and free energy varied separately). The decision uses the stronger: `Y_inoc = max(Y(d1E), Y(d1B))`. This is a screen on resource totals delivered as inoculum, not proof that no spatial resource effect exists; stated as a limit.
    52	
    53	Inoculum placement (all arms): a fixed footprint of `Nf` cells (disc around a fixed centre, raster order) chosen before any run. Candidate cells are those with `B=P=0`, no lineage, and E and S below `POOL_MAX` minus the per-cell share in **every** arm of the block; the inoculum `(B_r,E_r)` is split evenly over the first `Nmin..Nf` such cells (remainder to the first cells). Nothing is overwritten: only empty bound-matter cells receive B and E, and if fewer than `Nmin=12` common cells exist the block is `invalid-or-incomplete`, reported, and makes the candidate `invalid-or-incomplete` (blocks are not dropped; see 5.3). Every transferred state passes `validateState`, with pool capacity and combined lattice-plus-reserve matter limits checked before any unsigned-buffer write. The receiver genome is the receiver founder, lineage ids from the reserved set.
    54	
    55	### 4.3 Budget audit (`transfer.ts`)
    56	
    57	Every step produces a `TransferRecord`: `before` and `after` over **lattice plus reserve** (A,B,C,P,E,S, reserve B and E, matter, energy), `living`, `moved` (relabel counts), `converted` (P->C quanta and `heatAnalytic = (eP-eC)*P`, a named analytical heat term, cumulative), `reserveDebit`, `matterResidual = after.matter - before.matter` and `residual = (after.energy + heatAnalytic) - before.energy`; both must be exactly `0n` (BigInt). Placing the inoculum moves quanta from the reserve onto the lattice, so only the lattice-plus-reserve sum is conserved, never the lattice alone. The cumulative invariant of 4.1 holds at every census of both stages. A nonzero residual, an insufficient debit inventory or a failed `validateState` invalidates the block and fails the test. Starting totals per arm are published in evidence.
    58	
    59	### 4.4 Readouts (per arm, per block)
    60	
    61	Receiver survival (any receiver-lineage B at T2, and at each census); **receiver-labelled biomass inventory** (receiver-lineage B at T2) as the primary endpoint, named that way because transport assigns one genome to a destination's whole incoming bound mass including non-living material (`step.ts` reintegration), so labelled B can rise by takeover of existing detritus with no synthesis (fixture `takeover-only`). "Growth" is claimed only when flux attribution (below) is available and the synthesis share (PHOTO+GROW) of the receiver's gain is at least half; otherwise the edge text says "inventory benefit, growth not attributed". Builder cost: builder-lineage B, lattice/ledger E and A deltas and cumulative heat from census totals; **`kGene` maintenance is not separable** from catalytic and ordinary maintenance in `react` and is reported `unavailable`, not inferred. Inventories before/after each transfer from the audit record. **Spatial dependence** = (b) minus (c) receiver outcome. **Flux attribution** uses the four `roles` counters (PHOTO, GROW, DECOMP, RESP) read by a read-only observer at **every** Stage 2 step (so sampling is complete); each counter saturates at 65,535 in the reference step, so attribution is `unavailable` for a block if any counter reaches 65,535 at any step, with the reason recorded. There is no `mulShr` clamp flag and the plan does not pretend one exists. Census samples are never integrated as if they were every reaction.
    62	
    63	### 4.5 Naive-metric trap and fixtures
    64	
    65	Unit-test fixtures use 16x16 worlds with `kernelRadius` 5 (schema validation requires dimensions divisible by eight and `2*kernelRadius+1 <= min(tile)`; 12x12 and the default radius 9 are rejected, checked) and few steps so they stay in unit-test time. The positive control is additionally qualified at the frozen scientific scale (24x24, T2, injury regime) on dev (G3); a pass at unit scale does not qualify the full assay.

    53	    expect(r.map((x) => x.label)).toEqual(["supported", "unsupported-within-tested-domain", "unsupported-within-tested-domain"]);
    54	    expect(r[0].holmThreshold).toEqual({ num: 1, den: 60 });
    55	    // Holm stops at the first failure: b fails 1/40, so c is not rejected either although 9/256 <= 1/20.
    56	    expect(r[2].holmRejected).toBe(false);
    57	  });
    58	
    59	  it("a single candidate is tested at 1/20, so k=7 supports it and k=6 does not", () => {

    62	  });

// CPU reference implementation. This file is the specification of the rules:
// the WGSL kernels in @bl/sim-gpu must match it bit for bit.
//
// One step = affinity -> flow -> transport -> react.
//   affinity : U = G(K * (B+P); mu, sigma)            (Lenia growth field)
//   flow     : displacement = dt((1-a)grad U - a grad M + kAdhesion*grad P) + motility   (Flow-Lenia)
//              (adhesion is scaled by dt like the other two terms, unlike
//              motility; an attraction toward local polymer density, off by
//              default -- see WorldConfig.adhesion for what is and is not
//              demonstrated about its effect on cohesion)
//   transport: B, P, E and the genome move by exact integer reintegration;
//              A, C (membrane-gated) and S diffuse.
//   react    : controller + metabolism + decays + mutation; energy ledger.

import {
  CELL_CHANNELS,
  CH,
  G,
  GENOME_CHANNELS,
  B1_OFF,
  B2_OFF,
  NN_H,
  NN_I,
  NN_O,
  OUT,
  RND,
  U32_MAX,
  W1_OFF,
  W2_OFF,
  addu,
  SHAPE_BASE,
  buildKernel,
  buildShapeKernel,
  shapeRings,
  cellBase,
  cellCount,
  clampi,
  divi,
  divu,
  draw,
  lightModeId,
  mulFrac,
  muli,
  mulu,
  subu,
  worldW,
  NN_BYTES,
  LEDGER_MAX,
  MAX_STEP,
  POOL_MAX,
  DEFAULT_K_ADHESION,
  clampLesionRadius,
  mulShr,
  motilityTerm,
  validateState,
  FLUX_COUNT,
  FLUX_NAMES,
  ROLE_WORDS,
  encodeGenome,
  generalistGenome,
  packLineageLo,
  GENE_SHIFT,
  geneCount,
  geneMasks,
  popcount,
  type FluxName,
  type KernelTable,
  type ShapeKernel,
  type WorldConfig,
  type WorldState,
} from "@bl/schema";

export const MOT_ZERO = 128 | (128 << 8);
const FX = Object.fromEntries(FLUX_NAMES.map((k, i) => [k, i])) as Record<FluxName, number>;
const MCAP = 16383;

export interface MutationEvent {
  childHi: number;
  childLo: number;
  parentHi: number;
  parentLo: number;
}

export interface StepResult {
  events: MutationEvent[];
  light: bigint;
  heat: bigint;
}

export class RefSim {
  readonly cfg: WorldConfig;
  readonly n: number;
  readonly W: number;
  readonly H: number;
  readonly kernel: KernelTable;
  /** Ring-tagged kernel under WorldConfig.shapeReach (then also `kernel`), else null. */
  private readonly shape: ShapeKernel | null;
  state: WorldState;
  private cellsB: Uint32Array;
  private genomeB: Uint32Array;
  private U: Int32Array;
  private disp: Uint32Array;
  private readonly massDiv: number;
  /** Reference genome expressed by every cell in neutral-shadow runs. */
  private readonly refWords: Uint32Array | null;
  /** Diagnostic: last step's per-cell reaction amounts (see ROLE_WORDS). */
  readonly roles: Uint32Array;

  constructor(state: WorldState) {
    const errs = validateState(state);
    if (errs.length) throw new Error(`invalid state: ${errs.join("; ")}`);
    this.state = state;
    this.cfg = state.cfg;
    this.n = cellCount(this.cfg);
    this.W = worldW(this.cfg);
    this.H = this.n / this.W;
    this.shape = this.cfg.shapeReach === undefined ? null : buildShapeKernel(this.cfg.kernelRadius, this.cfg.shapeReach);
    this.kernel = this.shape ?? buildKernel(this.cfg.kernelRadius);
    this.cellsB = new Uint32Array(this.n * CELL_CHANNELS);
    this.genomeB = new Uint32Array(this.n * GENOME_CHANNELS);
    this.U = new Int32Array(this.n);
    this.disp = new Uint32Array(this.n);
    this.massDiv = 8 * this.cfg.massUnit;
    this.refWords = this.cfg.neutral ? encodeGenome(generalistGenome(this.cfg.defaultMu, this.cfg.defaultSigma), 0, 0) : null;
    this.roles = new Uint32Array(this.n * ROLE_WORDS);
  }

  /** Index of the neighbour of cell (x, y) at offset (dx, dy), wrapping inside its tile. */
  nb(x: number, y: number, dx: number, dy: number): number {
    const { tileW, tileH } = this.cfg;
import { cellCount, type WorldConfig } from "./config.ts";
import { CH, G, GENOME_CHANNELS } from "./layout.ts";
import type { WorldState } from "./world.ts";

export interface Totals {
  A: bigint;
  B: bigint;
  C: bigint;
  P: bigint;
  E: bigint;
  S: bigint;
  matter: bigint;
  /** Chemical potential + free pools + signal. */
  energy: bigint;
  living: number;
}

export function sumChannel(cells: Uint32Array, n: number, ch: number): bigint {
  let acc = 0;
  let big = 0n;
  const end = (ch + 1) * n;
  for (let i = ch * n; i < end; i++) {
    acc += cells[i];
    if (acc > 2 ** 50) {
      big += BigInt(acc);
      acc = 0;
    }
  }
  return big + BigInt(acc);
}

export function totalsOf(cfg: WorldConfig, cells: Uint32Array): Omit<Totals, "living"> {
  const n = cellCount(cfg);
  const A = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.A);
  const B = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.B);
  const C = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.C);
  const P = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.P);
  const E = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.E);
  const S = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.S);
  const energy = BigInt(cfg.eA) * A + BigInt(cfg.eB) * B + BigInt(cfg.eC) * C + BigInt(cfg.eP) * P + E + S;
  return { A, B, C, P, E, S, matter: A + B + C + P, energy };
}

/**
 * Energy ledger residual: (content now + heat exported) - (content at start + light absorbed).
 * Exactly zero when the rules conserve energy.
 */
export function ledgerResidual(start: { energy: bigint }, s: WorldState): bigint {
  const now = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
  return now.energy + s.heatOut - (start.energy + s.lightIn);
}

/** Order-dependent 64-bit digest of a word array, as 16 hex chars. */
export function digestWords(words: Uint32Array, h1 = 0x811c9dc5, h2 = 0x01000193): [number, number] {
  for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
    const w = words[i];
    h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ w, 0x85ebca6b) >>> 0;
    h1 = ((h1 << 13) | (h1 >>> 19)) >>> 0;
    h2 = Math.imul(h2 + w + i, 0xc2b2ae35) >>> 0;
    h2 = (h2 ^ (h2 >>> 16)) >>> 0;
  }
  return [h1, h2];
}

/**
 * Genome words of empty cells (lineage 0:0) are never read by the rules and
 * may hold stale data; the canonical form zeroes them.
 */
export function canonicalGenome(genome: Uint32Array): Uint32Array {
  const n = genome.length / GENOME_CHANNELS;
  let dirty = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < n && !dirty; i++)
    if ((genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) === 0)
      for (let g = 2; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) if (genome[g * n + i] !== 0) { dirty = true; break; }
  if (!dirty) return genome;
  const out = genome.slice();
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
    if ((out[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | out[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) === 0) for (let g = 2; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) out[g * n + i] = 0;
  return out;
}

/** Config serialised with sorted keys (stable across key order). */
export function canonicalConfig(c: WorldState["cfg"]): string {
  const o = c as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
  return JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(o).sort().map((k) => [k, o[k]])));
}

const u64Words = (v: bigint) => [Number(v & 0xffffffffn), Number((v >> 32n) & 0xffffffffn)];

function stateHashWords(s: WorldState): [number, number] {
  const cfg = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalConfig(s.cfg));
  const cfgWords = new Uint32Array(Math.ceil(cfg.length / 4));
  new Uint8Array(cfgWords.buffer).set(cfg);
  let [a, b] = digestWords(Uint32Array.of(cfg.length, ...cfgWords));
  [a, b] = digestWords(Uint32Array.of(s.step, ...u64Words(s.lightIn), ...u64Words(s.heatOut), ...s.flux.flatMap(u64Words)), a, b);
  [a, b] = digestWords(s.cells, a, b);
  [a, b] = digestWords(canonicalGenome(s.genome), a, b);
  return [a, b];
}

const hex = (a: number, b: number) => a.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") + b.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

/**
 * Canonical 64-bit digest of every persistent field: config, step, cells,
 * canonical genome and the full ledger (light, heat, fluxes). Physics-only —
 * used for pinned hashes, segment `startHash`/`startFrom` continuity checks
 * and same-device replay verification. Does not cover observer state; see
 * `artifactDigest` for the digest that does.
 */
export function stateHash(s: WorldState): string {
  const [a, b] = stateHashWords(s);
  return hex(a, b);
}

/**
 * Deterministic JSON for digesting: object keys sorted recursively (arrays
 * keep their order, since it's meaningful there). The observer payload is
 * caller-constructed, not arbitrary user JSON, so this only needs to be
 * stable across the field-insertion-order variance between callers, not
 * hostile input.
 */
function canonicalize(v: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(canonicalize);
  if (v && typeof v === "object") {
    const o = v as Record<string, unknown>;
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(o).sort().map((k) => [k, canonicalize(o[k])]));
  }
  return v;
}

/** Canonical JSON text of an observer payload, keys sorted recursively. */
export function canonicalObserverJSON(observer: unknown): string {
  return JSON.stringify(canonicalize(observer));
}

/**
 * Digest of a checkpoint artifact: `stateHash`'s digest words, chained with
 * the observer's canonical JSON bytes (same length-prefix + word-padding
 * pattern the config section uses). This is the digest checkpoint upload,
 * replay verification and the predecessor-start check all compare — it
 * replaces the old, separate physics `endHash` and `observer_hash` pair with
 * one value that covers the whole artifact. `startHash`/`startFrom`
 * continuity keeps using physics-only `stateHash`, unchanged.
 */
export function artifactDigest(s: WorldState, observer: unknown): string {
  let [a, b] = stateHashWords(s);
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalObserverJSON(observer));
  const words = new Uint32Array(Math.ceil(bytes.length / 4));
  new Uint8Array(words.buffer).set(bytes);
  // Framed as [length, ...words] via `.set()`, not `Uint32Array.of(len,
  // ...words)`: spreading `words` into call arguments blows the engine's
  // argument-count limit once an observer gets large (a populated tracker on
  // the 512x512 "large" preset is well past it), throwing "Maximum call
  // stack size exceeded" instead of ever producing a digest.
  const framed = new Uint32Array(1 + words.length);
  framed[0] = bytes.length;
  framed.set(words, 1);
  [a, b] = digestWords(framed, a, b);
  return hex(a, b);
}

    14	    <section class="intro">
    15	      <p class="eyebrow">First causal rung</p>
    16	      <h1>Does what a builder leaves behind help a receiver?</h1>
    17	      <p>A builder lineage conditions a small world: it lays down polymer and reshapes the nutrient, waste and energy around it. Then the builder is taken away, a receiver is placed on empty ground, and we ask whether the leftovers, and not something else, help it. Every transfer moves matter and energy by exact accounting, and the same receiver is also run against copies of the world that keep the totals but lose the structure, or give the receiver the energy alone.</p>
    18	      <p class="disclosure">Exploratory, CPU only, no GPU evidence, mutation off. A local experiment, kept off the public site. A negative here means "no causal benefit detected in this small panel, horizon and field", not that a second link is impossible.</p>
    19	    </section>
    20

    84	- `D_naive = Y(b | builder) - Y(d0)` (reported to show what the naive metric says; never gates). Co-culture (a) reported alongside and labelled as co-culture.
    85	
    86	### 5.3 Edge label (per candidate, on fresh blocks)
    87	- **Validity:** all 8 assigned fresh blocks must be valid with every ledger exactly zero; a placement or ledger failure makes the candidate `invalid-or-incomplete` (blocks are never dropped, because placement exclusions depend on the conditioned fields and dropping them would select on treatment).
    88	- **Per-contrast test:** exact one-sided sign test on the 8 paired differences, **zeros counted as non-positive** (conservative), `p = P(Binomial(8,1/2) >= k)` with `k` = number of strictly positive blocks: `k=8` gives 1/256, `k=7` gives 9/256. A contrast also needs a median per-block effect of at least `delta`.
    89	- **Candidate p-value:** intersection-union over the four gating contrasts: `p_cand = max` of the four p-values (all four must be significant at the candidate's threshold, no within-candidate correction needed). `delta` and the survival rule below are part of the same conjunction.
    90	- **Across candidates:** Holm over the (up to 3) candidates at alpha 0.05, thresholds 1/60, 1/40, 1/20 for 3 candidates (if candidates were dropped by the runtime rule, over those that ran). With 8 blocks the first rejection needs `k=8` in all four contrasts (1/256 < 1/60; `k=7` gives 9/256, above 1/60 and 1/40, below 1/20). Resolution is thereby stated, not hidden: this design can only certify a large, consistent effect.
    91	- **`delta`:** positive and set on dev only: `delta = max(ceil(0.02*B_r), 1.5 * max|D|)` over a **stage-2 replicate control** (same conditioned field, different Stage 2 PRNG seed, nonbuilder vs nonbuilder and builder vs builder); identical-twin replays always give exactly 0 and serve as correctness tests only, never as noise estimates. Estimating `max|D|` from 4 dev blocks is a stated crude floor.
    92	- **Survival:** the receiver survives in (b) in at least 7 of 8 fresh blocks.
    93	- **Labels:** `supported` iff all of the above hold. Mechanism text: "polymer-arrangement-attributed" only if `D_spaceP` also passes the sign test and delta while `D_spaceNP` does not; "non-polymer field pattern" if the reverse; otherwise "combined conditioned field". `unsupported-within-tested-domain` iff valid, measurement adequate and any gating test fails. `unsupported-measurement` iff the positive control (`hand-wall`) shows no effect in the frozen regime, or the builder produced no polymer at T1 (builder P total at or below the dev replicate floor) on dev. `invalid-or-incomplete` for ledger, placement, validation or funding failures.
    94	- **Panel verdict (three-valued):** any candidate `supported` => "causal benefit detected for pair X within this domain"; "no causal benefit detected" only when every candidate that ran was valid, adequately measured (positive control passed) and `unsupported-within-tested-domain`; any invalid or measurement-limited candidate yields an overall `invalid-or-incomplete` or `unsupported-measurement` verdict that names which candidates are negative and which are not usable.
    95

/**
 * A request's path, decoded and normalised (`.` and `..` segments resolved, repeated slashes folded, never above the
 * root), its raw form, and its query; null when the path does not decode. Every check below reads the normalised path,
 * so `//404.html`, `/./404.html` or `/x/../404.html` are the not-found page by name too, and no existence check looks
 * outside the root.
 */
function splitUrl(url = "/"): { pathname: string; decoded: string; path: string; search: string } | null {
  const q = url.indexOf("?");
  const [pathname, search] = q < 0 ? [url, ""] : [url.slice(0, q), url.slice(q)];
  try {
    const decoded = decodeURIComponent(pathname);
    return { pathname, decoded, path: posix.normalize(posix.join("/", decoded)), search };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * The not-found page asked for by name (`/404.html`, `/404.html/`, `/404%2ehtml`, `/404.HTML`): it answers 404 too, as in
 * Caddy, whose path matcher ignores case. Case is ignored here as well, since a case-insensitive disk (macOS) would
 * otherwise serve `/404.HTML` with 200.
 */
const namesNotFound = (path: string) => path.replace(/\/+$/, "").toLowerCase() === "/404.html";

/** The queries Vite's module loaders read (`?raw`, `?url`, `?import`, `?html-proxy` and the like). */
const VITE_MODULE_QUERY = /[?&](?:import|raw|url|inline|html-proxy|worker|sharedworker|direct)(?:[=&]|$)/;
/** Fetch destinations Vite's transform middleware never answers (vite 8, middlewares/transform.ts). */
const DOCUMENT_DESTS = new Set(["document", "iframe", "frame", "fencedframe"]);

/**
 * A module request for an .html file, which Vite's transform middleware answers as JavaScript: a module query, from a
 * fetch that is not a document's, that Vite would transform (a script fetch, an `import` query or an `html-proxy` one,
 * the same tests its middleware makes). Anything else asking for the page by name is a navigation or a plain fetch,
 * which a static server would answer with the file and 200, so it gets the page.
 */
function viteModuleRequest(req: Connect.IncomingMessage, search: string): boolean {
  const dest = req.headers["sec-fetch-dest"];
  if (typeof dest === "string" && DOCUMENT_DESTS.has(dest)) return false;
  if (!VITE_MODULE_QUERY.test(search)) return false;
  return dest === "script" || /[?&]import=?(?:&|$)/.test(search) || /[?&]html-proxy\b/.test(search);
}

/**
 * An unknown page answers 404 with the site's own page, 404.html, as deploy/Caddyfile does in production. Runs after
 * Vite's mpa html fallback, which has already rewritten a known page to its .html file. Only requests Vite's fallback
 * would treat as a page (GET or HEAD, with an Accept that names text/html or *\/* or is absent) get the page; a fetch
 * asking for JSON keeps Vite's empty 404. A directory with an index.html asked for without its slash answers 308 to
 * the slash, keeping the query, which is what Caddy's `redir` lines do for the eight top-level pages. Known differences
 * from Caddy: dev sends any such directory to its slash (`/garden`, which Caddy serves at 200 without one, and dev-only
{

    "build": "vite build",
    "preview": "vite preview"
  },

    "@plausible-analytics/tracker": "0.4.6"
  }
}
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import evidence from '../../../docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/evidence.json';
import { ARM_ORDER, ARM_TEXT, armRows, barWidth, edgeOf, esc, ledgerRows, ratText } from '../src/niche/render.ts';

it('formats rationals and escapes text', () => {
  expect(ratText({ num: 9, den: 256 })).toBe('9/256');
  expect(ratText({ num: 256, den: 256 })).toBe('1');
  expect(esc('<a href="x">&')).toBe('&lt;a href=&quot;x&quot;&gt;&amp;');
  expect(barWidth(50, 100, 100)).toBe(50);
  expect(barWidth(5, 0, 100)).toBe(0);
});

it('marks the dependency edge supported, unsupported or unavailable, never a failure as a negative', () => {
  expect(edgeOf('supported', 'combined conditioned field').state).toBe('supported');
  expect(edgeOf('unsupported-within-tested-domain', 'n/a').state).toBe('unsupported');
  for (const l of ['invalid-or-incomplete', 'unsupported-measurement', 'not-run']) expect(edgeOf(l, 'n/a').state).toBe('unavailable');
});

it('every arm has a plain-language name and description', () => {
  for (const id of ARM_ORDER) expect(ARM_TEXT[id].name.length).toBeGreaterThan(3);
});

it('renders the saved evidence: arms per candidate, an edge per candidate, zero residuals', () => {
  for (const c of evidence.fresh.candidates) {
    const rows = armRows(c.blocks as never, c.medianY);
    expect(rows.length).toBe(ARM_ORDER.length);
    expect(rows[0].perBlock.length).toBe(evidence.fresh.seeds.length);
    const r = evidence.fresh.results.find((x) => x.id === `cluster-${c.cluster}`)!;
    expect(['supported', 'unsupported', 'unavailable']).toContain(edgeOf(r.label, r.mechanism).state);
    const b0 = c.blocks[0] as never as { startTotals: never; transfers: never };
    for (const row of ledgerRows(b0.startTotals, b0.transfers)) expect(row.residual).toBe('0');
  }
});

   726	/** A ring's mean capped mass with 8 fractional bits: floor(conv * 256 / sum), 0 for an empty ring. */
   727	function ringMean(conv: number, sum: number): number {
   728	  if (sum === 0) return 0;
   729	  return addu(mulu(divu(conv, sum), 256), divu(mulu(conv % sum, 256), sum));
   730	}

     1	# I06 niche construction, first causal rung: result
     2	
     3	Exploratory, CPU only (sim-ref), mutation off, no physics change. Evidence: `evidence.json` (sealed), `manifest.json`, `calibration.json` (dev only), `protocol.lock.json` (written before the fresh run). Page: `apps/lab/niche/` (local only; 404 on the deployed site).
     4	
     5	## Result
     6	
     7	**No causal benefit detected** for any of the three builder candidates (M3 founder clusters 0, 4, 9 with the BUILD gene, receiver cluster 12), 8 fresh blocks each, T1 = 60 conditioning steps, T2 = 80 assay steps, 24x24 lattice with recurring injury and polymer shell. Candidate p = 255/256, 255/256, 1 (needed at most 1/60 after Holm); in every candidate the "inoculum matched" contrast is negative (the receiver does better when handed the matter or energy the field held than in the conditioned field), the polymer-ablation and spatial contrasts are not consistently positive, and the receiver survived in 8/8 blocks in every arm family.
     8	
     9	## What this does and does not say
    10	
    11	- The assay is not blind: the hand-built wall at 1200 polymer (about 10x what builders leave) was seen on dev (W2 floor under the receiver, 7/8 positive, p 9/256), so spatial structure can be detected at all. At builder scale (120) the same wall was not detected (W2 3/8, W1 4/8), and Stage-2 replicate noise sets delta at 291-404 against an inoculum of 480. A null for builder-scale polymer is therefore weak evidence of absence. Stated in the page and in `fresh.caveats`.
    12	- Builder polymer at the end of conditioning is only about 100-120 (adequate by the pre-stated floor of 10).
    13	- Flux attribution was available in every block (no role counter saturated); growth (synthesis at least half the gain) was attributed in 1, 1 and 2 of 8 builder-removed blocks, so the receiver-biomass differences are mostly inventory, not growth.
    14	- Builder cost is reported from census totals; `kGene` maintenance is `unavailable` (not separable in the reference step).
    15	- Negative fixture: energy-gift worlds (uniform, inside the footprint, in the halo annulus) are fooled by the naive metric (8/8 blocks positive) and caught by the scramble (uniform) or the energy-matched inoculum (footprint, annulus); takeover-only raises receiver-labelled inventory with zero synthesis; the null field gives exact zeros; the four placement/pool/underfunding guards refuse as designed.
    16	- Exploratory, one receiver genome, one field per candidate; not part of the frozen preregistration; not a statement that a second link is impossible.
    17	
    18	## Pre-stated rules and implementation amendments
    19	
    20	Decision rules are PLAN 5; they were not changed. Amendments made before any fresh run: d1B bound matter is B+P (see REVIEW finding 1); the positive control uses 8 dev wall seeds (an exact sign test over 4 blocks cannot reach 1/20); the hand-wall is qualified with injury on (the floor protects only under `injuryShell`) and at 10x builder polymer; the bound `PROTOCOL.delta` values are the dev replicate floors, checked by `--check`. After the Sol source review the evidence was regenerated (REVIEW.md); the verdict did not change.
    21	
    22	## Commands and results (this worktree, Node 24.2, machine at load average about 60)
    23	
    24	```

  step(): StepResult {
    if (this.state.step >= MAX_STEP) throw new Error(`step limit ${MAX_STEP} reached`);
    this.affinity();
    this.flow();
    this.transport();
    const r = this.react();
    this.state.step += 1;
    if ([this.state.lightIn, this.state.heatOut, ...this.state.flux].some((v) => v >= LEDGER_MAX))
      throw new Error("ledger total reached 2^63; the run cannot continue exactly");
    return r;
  }

  run(steps: number): MutationEvent[] {
    const ev: MutationEvent[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) for (const e of this.step().events) ev.push(e);
    return ev;
  }

  private mass(cells: Uint32Array, i: number): number {
    const m = cells[CH.B * this.n + i] + cells[CH.P * this.n + i];

   195	/** Block, step and cost accounting of one regeneration (informational; timings are measured by the tool, never sealed). */
   196	export function stepProjection(p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): { formula: string; blocks: number; steps: number } {
   197	  const blocks = p.panel.length * (p.devSeeds.length + p.freshSeeds.length);
   198	  const devExtra = p.panel.length * p.devSeeds.length * 2 * p.T2;
   199	  const perBlock = 2 * p.T1 + 10 * p.T2;
   200	  return { formula: "blocks * (2*T1 + 10*T2) + devBlocks * 2*T2 + wall runs", blocks, steps: blocks * perBlock + devExtra + 4 * 2 * p.wallSeeds.length * p.T2 };
   201	}

    99	export function devReport(p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): DevReport {
   100	  const candidates: DevCandidate[] = p.panel.map((cluster) => {
   101	    const blocks = p.devSeeds.map((s, i) => compactBlock(runBlock(cluster, s, p, p.devReplicateSeeds[i])));
   102	    const rep = blocks.flatMap((b) => (b.contrasts ? [Math.abs(b.contrasts.replicateBuilder ?? 0), Math.abs(b.contrasts.replicateNonbuilder ?? 0)] : []));
   103	    const pMed = median(blocks.map((b) => Number(b.costs.find((c) => c.kind === "builder")?.P ?? 0)));
   104	    return { cluster, blocks, replicateAbs: rep, deltaProposed: deltaOf(p.reserve.B, rep), builderPMedian: pMed, polymerAdequate: pMed > p.polymerFloor };
   105	  });
   106	  const W1 = wallReport("W1", p.wall.totalP, p), W2 = wallReport("W2", p.wall.totalP, p);
   107	  const bs = { W1: wallReport("W1", p.wall.builderScaleP, p), W2: wallReport("W2", p.wall.builderScaleP, p) };
   108	  return { purpose: "calibration", seeds: p.devSeeds, candidates, wall: { W1, W2, builderScale: bs, positiveControlPassed: W1.passes || W2.passes, used: W2.passes ? "W2" : W1.passes ? "W1" : null } };
   109	}

   209	export function placeInoculum(s: WorldState, a: Audit, cells: readonly number[], words: Uint32Array, allowP = false): TransferRecord {
   210	  return audited("place-inoculum", s, a, () => {
   211	    const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   212	    const m = cells.length;
   213	    if (m === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("no cell to place the inoculum on");
   214	    const bBase = Math.floor(a.reserveB / m), bRem = a.reserveB % m;
   215	    const eBase = Math.floor(a.reserveE / m), eRem = a.reserveE % m;
   216	    const perE = eBase + (eRem > 0 ? 1 : 0);
   217	    for (const i of cells) if (!placeable(s, i, perE, allowP)) throw new NicheInvalid(`placement refused: cell ${i} is occupied or at its pool cap`);
   218	    const B = a.reserveB, E = a.reserveE;
   219	    cells.forEach((i, j) => {
   220	      s.cells[CH.B * n + i] += bBase + (j < bRem ? 1 : 0);
   221	      s.cells[CH.E * n + i] += eBase + (j < eRem ? 1 : 0);
   222	      for (let g = 0; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) s.genome[g * n + i] = words[g];
   223	    });
   224	    a.reserveB = 0;
   225	    a.reserveE = 0;
   226	    return { placedCells: m, placedB: B, placedE: E };
    51	      }
    52	    }

codex
**P1**

None confirmed. The vacuous-negative-fixture class was checked and held up: all energy-gift variants fool the naive metric in 8/8 blocks. Uniform gifts fail the spatial contrast; footprint gifts have `D_inoc=0`; annulus gifts have negative `D_inoc`. The matched controls genuinely catch them. Conservation residuals are computed from actual inventories and ledgers.

**P2**

- **Polymer adequacy differs from the specification.** [evidence.ts:104](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/packages/runner/src/niche/evidence.ts:104) uses fixed `polymerFloor=10`; [PLAN.md:93](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/PLAN.md:93) requires the dev replicate floor. No separate polymer replicate floor is calculated. Current P medians `122/112/97` are below the documented replicate floors `341/291/404`, yet all candidates become adequately measured negatives. **Fix:** explicitly define and reconcile the adequacy rule; freeze any amendment before using new confirmation seeds.

- **Co-culture flux attribution drops reactions before injury clears lineage.** [assay.ts:58](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/packages/runner/src/niche/assay.ts:58) attributes roles using post-reaction ownership. Cluster 4, seed `8600105`, reports synthesis **26**; recording producing-cell ownership before reaction gives **27**, without saturation and with the identical final state hash. **Fix:** mark co-culture attribution unavailable until producing ownership can be observed accurately. Receiver-only attribution held up.

- **Unavailable evidence crashes the page.** [page.ts:30](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/apps/lab/src/niche/page.ts:30) assumes a candidate exists, although failed G3 produces `fresh.candidates=[]`. An all-invalid candidate instead crashes at `fmt(null)` on line 39. Both paths reproduced in memory. **Fix:** render stopped/invalid results explicitly, handle absent measurements, and test the complete page for these payloads.

- **Displayed medians can reverse comparisons.** [evidence.ts:50](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/packages/runner/src/niche/evidence.ts:50) selects the upper middle observation, despite promising the lower middle and displaying “median.” For cluster 4, `cNP` displays **363** versus builder **288**; conventional medians are **280.5** versus **285.5**, reversing their order. **Fix:** retain twice-the-median integers and divide when rendering, or consistently label the chosen order statistic.

- **Sealed evidence omits required transfer inventories.** [evidence.ts:43](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/packages/runner/src/niche/evidence.ts:43) strips before/after snapshots, retaining them only for `b`, `d1E`, and `d1B`. The ablation’s P→C transformation therefore publishes residuals and conversion details without its before/after inventory; other reconstruction arms also lose theirs. **Fix:** retain complete transfer records for every arm as PLAN §4.3–4.4 requires.

**P3**

- **The exhausted-reserve fixture tests the wrong refusal.** [transfer.ts:213](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/packages/runner/src/niche/transfer.ts:213) accepts a spent reserve on different empty cells. Reproduction returned zero deposited B/E, valid state, zero residual, and increased living labels from **13 to 21**. [fixtures.ts:202](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/packages/runner/src/niche/fixtures.ts:202) retries occupied cells, masking this. **Fix:** reject exhausted reserves and test disjoint empty destinations.

- **Survival prose contradicts the evidence.** [EVIDENCE.md:7](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/EVIDENCE.md:7) claims 8/8 survival in every arm family. Cluster 4 has extinction in `c`, seed `8600103`, and `cNP`, seed `8600107`; each survives 7/8. **Fix:** report arm-specific survival.

- **Runtime projection omits 14,400 steps.** [evidence.ts:200](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/packages/runner/src/niche/evidence.ts:200) excludes `5 fixtures × 8 seeds × 9 arms × 40 steps`, reporting **40,160** instead of **54,560**. **Fix:** include fixture execution in the projection and runtime gate.

- **The new packaging test loosens the timeout.** [niche-packaging.test.ts:56](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-niche-construction/apps/lab/test/niche-packaging.test.ts:56) overrides the suite’s 60-second limit with 120 seconds. A 90-second build consequently passes despite the explicit constraint. **Fix:** retain the existing limit or run packaging as a separate build gate.
